Major cities generally contain specialized Entheon medical facilities, while smaller settlements may rely on local clinics or refer patients to larger centres. Entheon healthcare is treated as a professional medical field rather than simply an extension of Trainer services.

14.4 Capture Crystal Services

Capture Crystals are widely available through authorized retailers and specialist suppliers. Services associated with Capture Crystals may include:

  • purchasing new Capture Crystals
  • Crystal identification
  • maintenance and inspection
  • repair of damaged equipment
  • registration services
  • specialized Crystal fitting and configuration

Standard Capture Crystals are affordable enough for ordinary Trainers, while specialized or high-performance models can be significantly more expensive. Capture Crystal technology is regulated, particularly where modifications could affect Entheon safety or violate established capture laws.

14.5 Accommodation and Hospitality

Kaleo has a wide range of accommodation options. Depending on the location, travellers may find:

  • hotels
  • guesthouses
  • hostels
  • inns
  • private rentals
  • resorts
  • campsites

Many establishments are accustomed to accommodating Trainers and their Entheon. Some provide dedicated Entheon facilities such as:

  • secure resting areas
  • food preparation areas
  • outdoor exercise spaces
  • grooming facilities
  • temporary Entheon accommodation

Major cities and tourist destinations generally offer the widest range of accommodation.

14.6 Food and Restaurants

Food culture varies significantly between regions. Restaurants, cafés, markets, food stalls and other establishments are common throughout Kaleo. Trainers travelling with Entheon can generally purchase appropriate food for themselves and their partners, although specialized Entheon diets may require visiting dedicated suppliers. Regional cuisine reflects local ingredients, climate, culture and economic activity.

14.7 Trainer Services

Larger settlements commonly provide specialized services aimed at Trainers. These may include:

  • training facilities
  • public battle arenas
  • private practice facilities
  • Trainer coaching
  • Entheon grooming
  • equipment rental
  • specialized training programs
  • performance and conditioning services

Some services are highly specialized and may only be available in major cities. Trainers are not required to use professional facilities. Public spaces and natural environments can also be used for training where local regulations permit it.

14.8 Financial Services

Modern financial services are widely available. People may use:

  • banks
  • ATMs
  • digital payment systems
  • electronic transfers
  • payment cards
  • mobile payment services

Trainers can receive prize money, wages and other payments through standard financial services. Major cities contain larger financial institutions and more specialized services, while smaller communities generally provide basic banking access.

14.9 Communication Services

Kaleo has advanced communication infrastructure. People commonly use mobile devices and other connected technology for:

  • communication
  • navigation
  • banking
  • entertainment
  • emergency services
  • Trainer records
  • accessing public information

Communication coverage is extensive around developed areas but can become less reliable in remote wilderness.

14.10 Regional Availability

Services scale with settlement size and importance. Major cities can provide almost any ordinary commercial or professional service. Towns generally provide essential services along with a selection of specialized businesses. Villages and small settlements focus primarily on everyday necessities. Remote communities may have only limited shops and services and can rely on larger settlements for specialized goods, medical care, advanced equipment and other needs. This variation means that Trainers travelling through Kaleo need to plan ahead when entering remote areas.

15. Travel & Transportation

Travel throughout Kaleo is shaped by the size of the settlements, the established transportation network and the distance between developed areas. People travel regularly between regions for work, education, business, recreation and personal reasons. Trainers are among the people most likely to spend extended periods travelling.

15.1 Everyday Travel

Within established settlements, most people travel on foot, by bicycle, or using the available local transportation systems. Urban environments are generally designed to make walking and public transportation practical. The exact transportation options vary between settlements according to their size, geography and local infrastructure.

15.2 Intercity Travel

Travelling between major settlements is a normal part of life. Established transportation connections allow people and goods to move efficiently between population centres, while smaller communities may have fewer direct connections. Travel does not follow a single predetermined route through Kaleo. People choose routes according to their destination, available connections, personal preferences and local conditions.

15.3 Trainer Travel

Trainers travelling between settlements generally make use of the same transportation systems as other members of society. A Trainer may travel by public transportation, use established trails, arrange private transportation or combine several methods during a journey. Capture Crystals make travelling with multiple Entheon considerably more practical, allowing partnered Entheon to accompany their Trainer without requiring each individual Entheon to physically travel alongside them at all times. Trainers must still consider the needs of their Entheon during extended journeys, including food, exercise, healthcare and opportunities for rest.

15.4 Travel Planning

Long-distance travel requires more preparation than travelling between nearby settlements. Trainers may need to consider:

  • available accommodation
  • food and supplies
  • Entheon care
  • medical access
  • communication coverage
  • weather and environmental conditions
  • distance between settlements
  • available transportation connections

Experienced Trainers generally become accustomed to planning around these practical considerations.

15.5 Wilderness Travel

Travelling away from established settlements and routes is substantially different from ordinary civilian travel. Remote areas can involve:

  • difficult terrain
  • changing weather
  • limited communications
  • wild Entheon
  • limited access to medical facilities
  • long distances between settlements

Advanced technology provides useful tools for navigation, communication and safety, but it does not eliminate the challenges created by natural environments. Trainers who deliberately explore remote areas generally prepare more extensively than travellers following established routes.

15.6 Entheon-Assisted Travel

Some Entheon can naturally assist with transportation or logistics. Depending on their species and capabilities, Entheon may be able to assist with:

  • carrying equipment
  • crossing difficult terrain
  • travelling through water
  • reaching elevated locations
  • transporting small loads
  • other tasks suited to their natural abilities

Such use depends on the individual Entheon and the circumstances. Entheon are partners and living creatures rather than disposable transportation equipment. Their welfare, willingness and physical capabilities must be considered when using their abilities for travel.

15.7 Travel Between Regions

Regional travel is common, and people regularly move between different parts of Kaleo. Major cities provide important connections between regions, while smaller communities may depend on connections through larger population centres. Maritime travel is particularly important for communities located around the Central Sea and other significant bodies of water. The established regional map and individual regional entries determine the specific routes, settlements and geographical features encountered during travel.

15.8 Travel and the Trainer Journey

A Trainer’s journey is not limited to Gym challenges. Trainers may travel for many reasons, including:

  • visiting friends and family
  • exploring unfamiliar regions
  • studying Entheon
  • participating in competitions
  • visiting landmarks
  • pursuing work or research
  • seeking particular Entheon
  • experiencing different communities and cultures

The Gym challenge can therefore form one part of a much larger journey through Kaleo rather than dictating the route a Trainer must take.

16. Education & Everyday Life

Education and ordinary civilian life in Kaleo are not centered around Entheon battling. Although Entheon are deeply integrated into society, most people grow up, study, work and socialize without pursuing a professional Trainer career. Entheon are nevertheless a familiar part of everyday life, and most people encounter them from an early age.

16.1 Education System

Kaleo has a developed education system covering childhood education, secondary education, vocational training and higher education. Schools provide a broad general education while also allowing students to develop interests in specialized fields. Common areas of education include:

  • mathematics
  • sciences
  • languages
  • history
  • geography
  • arts
  • technology
  • physical education
  • environmental studies
  • Entheon studies

Schools make use of advanced educational technology and AI-assisted tools, while teachers remain responsible for guiding students and providing human instruction. Higher education includes universities, research institutions, technical schools and vocational programs.

16.2 Entheon Education

Basic knowledge about Entheon is a normal part of education. Students learn about subjects such as:

  • Entheon biology
  • basic behaviour and communication
  • habitat and environmental awareness
  • Entheon welfare
  • safe interaction with wild Entheon
  • Affinity fundamentals
  • responsible Entheon ownership

The purpose of this education is not to turn every child into a Trainer. Understanding how to behave around Entheon is considered useful general knowledge for anyone living in Kaleo.

16.3 Children and Entheon

Children commonly grow up around Entheon. Some families own partnered Entheon, while others do not. A child having regular contact with an Entheon does not automatically make them a Trainer. Entheon ownership and formal Trainer activities remain separate from ordinary childhood interaction. Children are generally taught to respect Entheon as living beings and to understand that unfamiliar wild Entheon should not be approached recklessly. Parents and guardians remain responsible for ensuring that children interact safely with Entheon.

16.4 Becoming a Trainer

There is no requirement for every young person to become a Trainer. Those who wish to pursue formal Trainer activities can receive additional education and practical instruction. This may include:

  • Entheon care
  • battle safety
  • training principles
  • Capture Crystal use
  • wilderness safety
  • first aid
  • responsible interaction with wild Entheon
  • Trainer regulations

Some young people begin their Trainer journey through a regional research centre and its Starter Program, while others become Trainers later in life. There is no single age at which everyone becomes a Trainer.

16.5 Family and Household Life

Entheon can be ordinary members of a household. Families may keep Entheon primarily as companions, while others may have Entheon that assist with work, recreation or household activities. The relationship between a family and its Entheon varies considerably. Some Entheon are deeply involved in family life and spend most of their time around their human companions. Others may have more independent routines while remaining part of the household. Caring for an Entheon is a responsibility rather than simply an ownership status. Food, healthcare, exercise, enrichment and appropriate living conditions are part of responsible Entheon care.

16.6 Work and Daily Life

Most adults in Kaleo have ordinary careers unrelated to professional battling. People work in fields ranging from education and healthcare to science, technology, agriculture, manufacturing, retail, hospitality, administration and entertainment. Entheon may be present in workplaces where their abilities or companionship are appropriate. Some professions make extensive use of Entheon, while others involve little or no interaction with them. The presence of Entheon therefore does not make ordinary human labour obsolete. Instead, humans and Entheon have developed many different forms of cooperation.

16.7 Leisure and Entertainment

Kaleans have a wide range of leisure activities. People may spend their free time:

  • socializing with friends
  • playing sports
  • gaming
  • watching entertainment
  • attending concerts and performances
  • visiting restaurants and cafés
  • travelling
  • exploring nature
  • participating in hobbies
  • attending local events
  • watching Entheon battles

Entheon battling is one form of entertainment among many. Casual and competitive battles can attract spectators, while local festivals may incorporate exhibitions, demonstrations and community competitions.

16.8 Festivals and Community Life

Communities throughout Kaleo have their own festivals, celebrations and traditions. These can include:

  • seasonal festivals
  • harvest celebrations
  • cultural events
  • city anniversaries
  • local sporting events
  • Entheon-related celebrations
  • community competitions
  • religious or historical observances where locally appropriate

The specific traditions of a community depend on its history, environment and culture. Entheon may participate in celebrations alongside humans, particularly where their presence has become part of local tradition.

16.9 Entheon in Everyday Society

Entheon occupy many different roles in Kalean society. They may be:

  • household companions
  • working partners
  • research subjects
  • conservation partners
  • medical or therapeutic partners
  • professional competitors
  • recreational partners
  • participants in performances and entertainment

These roles are not mutually exclusive, and an individual Entheon may have a relationship with humans that does not fit neatly into any single category. An Entheon being capable of battling does not mean battling is its primary purpose.

16.10 Everyday Attitudes Toward Entheon

Entheon are generally treated as a normal part of life rather than as extraordinary creatures. People can have very different personal attitudes toward them. Some people are passionate Trainers. Some enjoy Entheon as companions. Some work closely with them professionally. Others have little interest in Entheon beyond ordinary daily interactions. This allows Entheon to remain an important part of Kalean society without making them the sole focus of human life.

17. Law, Safety & Public Rules

Kaleo is a modern society governed by established laws and institutions. Entheon are deeply integrated into everyday life, and the legal system therefore includes specific protections and regulations concerning Entheon, Trainers, Capture Crystals and Entheon-related activities. These rules exist alongside ordinary civilian law.

17.1 General Law

Kalean law covers the same broad areas expected in a modern society, including:

  • personal safety
  • property
  • contracts
  • fraud
  • theft
  • assault
  • public safety
  • environmental protection
  • commercial activity

Entheon do not exist outside the normal legal framework. Crimes committed using Entheon are still subject to ordinary criminal law, with additional considerations where the welfare or agency of an Entheon is involved.

17.2 Trainer Responsibilities

Registered Trainers are responsible for the Entheon under their care. This includes providing appropriate:

  • food
  • shelter
  • healthcare
  • exercise
  • enrichment
  • supervision

A Trainer is also responsible for taking reasonable precautions when using Entheon in public or allowing them to interact with other people and Entheon. Trainer registration and the associated TraID provide the legal foundation for recognizing Trainer and Entheon ownership.

17.3 Entheon Welfare and Protection

Entheon are recognized as sentient beings with individual agency. Their welfare is protected by law, including protections against unnecessary cruelty, neglect, exploitation and abuse. Entheon used for work, research, entertainment or battling remain entitled to appropriate care. The existence of an Entheon partnership does not remove the Entheon’s individual welfare considerations.

17.4 Capture and Ownership

Capture Crystals and Entheon ownership are regulated. Registered Capture Crystals are linked to their legal owner, and captured Entheon are associated with both the Crystal and their Trainer’s TraID. This system provides the foundation for:

  • ownership
  • transfers
  • trading
  • identifying stolen Entheon
  • investigating illegal captures

Unauthorized capture, theft, trafficking and deliberate attempts to circumvent the registration system are subject to legal consequences.

17.5 Public Battling

Entheon battles are a normal part of Kalean society. Registered Trainers may participate in roadside battles, friendly battles, Gym challenges, tournaments and other recognized forms of competition. Battles in public spaces must be conducted in a manner that does not unnecessarily endanger bystanders, property or uninvolved Entheon. A Trainer is not required to accept a battle challenge. Declining a challenge is not inherently suspicious or dishonorable.

17.6 Battle Safety

Entheon combat has an inherent limitation:

An Entheon cannot kill another Entheon through direct combat.

Battles can nevertheless result in knockouts, exhaustion, temporary injuries, serious non-lethal injuries and the need for medical treatment or recovery. This limitation is an inherent rule of Entheon combat rather than merely a regulation imposed by the League or other competitive organizations. Entheon can still die from illness, natural causes, accidents, environmental hazards and circumstances unrelated to direct Entheon combat.

17.7 Wild Entheon

Wild Entheon remain part of Kaleo’s natural ecosystems and are subject to environmental and conservation protections. Trainers and other individuals are expected to respect:

  • protected habitats
  • conservation areas
  • breeding populations
  • restricted ecological zones
  • local wildlife regulations

Interfering with wild Entheon populations, destroying habitats or capturing Entheon in prohibited circumstances may constitute a legal offense. Specific restrictions can vary according to the region and the ecological importance of an area.

17.8 Criminal Use of Entheon

Using Entheon to commit crimes is illegal. This can include:

  • assault
  • theft
  • intimidation
  • smuggling
  • illegal trafficking
  • property destruction
  • coercion
  • unauthorized capture or transfer

The fact that an Entheon is capable of causing significant damage does not place responsibility solely on the Entheon. Human actions and the circumstances surrounding the incident are considered when determining responsibility.

17.9 Rogue Trainers

A person who possesses or trains captured Entheon without maintaining the legally required Trainer registration may be referred to as a rogue Trainer. Being a rogue Trainer does not automatically make someone a criminal. However, rogue Trainers may attract additional scrutiny because unregistered Entheon can be associated with stolen Entheon, illegal trafficking, underground battling, concealed identities or deliberate avoidance of legal oversight. “Rogue Trainer” is primarily a common social term rather than necessarily a formal criminal classification.

17.10 Law Enforcement and Emergency Services

Kaleo has ordinary law enforcement and emergency services operating throughout its settlements. Depending on the situation, authorities may respond to incidents involving:

  • criminal activity
  • dangerous Entheon
  • accidents
  • missing persons
  • environmental emergencies
  • public safety threats
  • illegal capture or trafficking

Emergency medical services can treat both humans and Entheon, with specialized Entheon medical facilities available in larger settlements.

17.11 Public Safety and Responsibility

Advanced technology and the widespread presence of Entheon make public safety a shared responsibility. Individuals are expected to use appropriate judgment when:

  • bringing Entheon into public spaces
  • battling near other people
  • travelling through populated areas
  • interacting with unfamiliar Entheon
  • exploring wilderness
  • using specialized Affinity abilities or equipment

The law provides the framework for dealing with dangerous situations, but ordinary caution and responsible behaviour remain an important part of everyday life.

17.12 Relationship Between Trainer Institutions and Civilian Law

Gyms, research centres and League organizations operate within the broader Kalean legal system. They may establish additional rules governing their own activities, competitions or facilities, but these rules do not replace ordinary civilian law.

The species entries below are the authoritative visual and biological reference for Entheon species. Individual specimens may vary naturally, but the listed measurements establish the normal reference scale for narrative description and visualisation.

Species Entry Framework

Each species entry is structured around the following core categories:

  1. Species concept — core identity and archetype.
  2. Anatomy & appearance — body plan, proportions, head, limbs, wings/tail and distinctive anatomy.
  3. Surface & coloration — material/texture, primary colours, secondary colours and markings.
  4. Affinity manifestation — how each affinity appears biologically, visually and functionally.
  5. Personality & behaviour — temperament, habits and ecological behaviour.
  6. Combat identity — broad fighting style and characteristic affinity use.
  7. Evolution progression — how each stage develops from the previous one.
  8. Silhouette priority — the most important visual feature for recognition.

Not every species requires every category to be stated separately; descriptions prioritize the information most relevant to that species.

Global design rules

  • These are original species, not renamed copies of existing Pokémon.
  • Existing creatures may inspire broad archetypes or body plans, but each final design needs its own identity.
  • Evolution stages should remain recognizably related while becoming meaningfully more developed.
  • Normal three-stage lines retain their established affinities from stage 2 into stage 3.
  • Branching evolution may intentionally replace the base affinity.
  • Affinity should influence anatomy, behaviour and abilities without reducing every species to an elemental cliché.
  • Common species may be simple; rare and Legendary species may be more elaborate.
  • Legendary status does not automatically mean enormous size.
  • No creature should accidentally duplicate another creature’s primary silhouette or ecological niche.
  • Written descriptions establish the intended canonical species identity, anatomy, behaviour and Affinity expression.

Starter Line 1 — Nimblet → Nymbril → Nymbrake

Affinity: Wild → Wild/Gale → Wild/Gale Evolution: Three-stage Role: Agile Wild starter line

Size & Physical Scale

Each Entheon species has a typical adult physical scale. Individual specimens naturally vary, but the measurements below establish the normal reference point for visual description and narrative comparison.

  • Average Height: Standing height for bipeds; shoulder height for quadrupeds; maximum natural body height for serpentine, floating or otherwise non-standard body plans.
  • Average Length: Front of the body to the end of the body or tail in a natural resting posture. Wings, extended limbs and other temporary projections are excluded.
  • Size Class: A human-relative reference based primarily on the creature’s established height and overall physical presence.

For quadrupedal Entheon, shoulder height must not be interpreted as total head height. A large bear-, rhino- or dragon-like creature can therefore have a shoulder height below a human’s standing height while still being substantially larger and heavier than the human.

Size Classes

Size Class General Reference
Tiny Smaller than a domestic cat
Small Cat-sized to roughly knee height on an adult human
Medium Roughly knee height to waist height on an adult human
Large Roughly waist height to human height, or a substantially built quadruped of comparable physical scale
Very Large Human-sized to clearly towering or exceptionally massive
Huge Clearly larger than a human-scale creature, with exceptional overall physical presence

These measurements are intended as species-level averages rather than hard limits.

Nimblet

Average Height: .45 m Average Length: .75 m Size Class: Small

Species concept: Small monkey-like creature. Appearance:

  • Small, compact primate body.
  • Short muzzle and rounded ears.
  • Expressive eyes and an inquisitive face.
  • Long arms and legs suited for climbing and rapid movement.
  • A long, flexible, partially prehensile tail.
  • Small hands and feet with dexterous fingers and toes.
  • Soft, short fur.
  • Earthy/natural coloration, avoiding a strongly fox-like appearance.
  • Overall silhouette should immediately read as a young monkey rather than a fox, cat, or conventional rodent.

Behaviour:

  • Curious, energetic and highly inquisitive.
  • Constantly investigates unfamiliar objects and environments.
  • Playful and prone to climbing anything it can reach.
  • Can be mischievous without being malicious.
  • Highly adaptable and quick to learn from its surroundings.

Wild affinity manifestation:

  • Exceptional natural agility.
  • Strong climbing ability.
  • Rapid reactions and instinctive movement.
  • Uses its environment creatively rather than relying purely on brute strength.

Combat identity:

  • Fast, evasive and unpredictable.
  • Uses rapid strikes, jumps and acrobatic movement.
  • Prefers mobility and positioning over direct confrontation.

Nymbril

Average Height: .85 m Average Length: 1.25 m Size Class: Medium

Species concept: Adolescent/young adult simian developing into an agile aerial fighter. Appearance:

  • Taller and more athletic than Nimblet.
  • Longer limbs with noticeably greater muscular definition.
  • More developed hands and feet.
  • Longer, stronger prehensile tail.
  • Slightly larger ears and more pronounced facial structure.
  • Fur becomes somewhat denser around the shoulders, arms and tail.
  • Body remains recognizably simian and clearly related to Nimblet.
  • Subtle Gale-associated visual features can begin appearing around the limbs and tail.

Behaviour:

  • More confident and adventurous.
  • Still playful, but increasingly competitive.
  • Enjoys testing its agility against other creatures.
  • Frequently climbs to high vantage points.
  • Becomes protective of younger members of its group.

Wild affinity manifestation:

  • Greater physical adaptability and agility.
  • Can use branches, rocks and terrain as part of its movement and combat.

Gale affinity manifestation:

  • Uses bursts of wind to extend jumps.
  • Can redirect itself while airborne.
  • Creates short gusts through rapid movement.
  • Begins developing the aerial mobility that defines its final evolution.

Combat identity:

  • Highly mobile skirmisher.
  • Combines physical strikes with short Gale bursts.
  • Difficult to pin down.
  • Excels at hit-and-move tactics.

Nymbrake

Average Height: 1.45 m Average Length: 2.10 m Size Class: Large

Species concept: Powerful adult simian combining Wild instinct with Gale-enhanced movement. Appearance:

  • Large, athletic primate with a powerful but agile build.
  • Long, muscular arms and legs.
  • Broad shoulders and strong upper body.
  • Long, highly flexible prehensile tail that can function almost like an additional limb.
  • Larger hands and feet with powerful gripping ability.
  • More pronounced facial structure and mature features.
  • Dense fur around the shoulders, forearms and tail.
  • Gale energy can manifest visibly around its limbs and tail during movement.
  • Overall silhouette should feel powerful without becoming a generic muscular ape.

Behaviour:

  • Confident and highly intelligent.
  • Protective of its territory and companions.
  • Enjoys difficult physical challenges.
  • Has a strong instinct for improvisation and environmental exploitation.
  • Can appear playful outside combat but becomes intensely focused when challenged.

Wild affinity manifestation:

  • Exceptional physical coordination.
  • Strong instincts and environmental awareness.
  • Can adapt its fighting style rapidly.

Gale affinity manifestation:

  • Uses powerful gusts to propel itself through the air.
  • Can make enormous leaps.
  • Can alter its trajectory mid-air.
  • Rapid limb movement can generate concentrated bursts of wind.
  • Its fighting style revolves around using momentum and aerial movement.

Combat identity:

  • Fast physical attacker.
  • Combines powerful melee strikes with aerial mobility.
  • Uses Gale to amplify jumps, dodges and attacks.
  • Can turn the environment itself into part of its fighting style.

Evolution progression:

Nimblet: Curious little primate → Nymbril: Agile developing climber → Nymbrake: Powerful Wild/Gale aerial fighter

Starter Line 2 — Pipiri → Pirello → Piravelle

Affinity: Tide → Tide/Frost → Tide/Frost Evolution: Three-stage Role: Aquatic/amphibious Tide starter line

Pipiri

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .55 m Size Class: Small

Species concept: Small axolotl-like amphibious creature. Appearance:

  • Small, rounded amphibious body.
  • Four short legs.
  • Broad, soft tail designed for swimming.
  • Prominent external feathery gills on either side of the head.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Small rounded mouth.
  • Smooth, slightly rubbery-looking skin.
  • Small fin-like ridges along parts of the back.
  • Compact, cute proportions.
  • Clearly aquatic/amphibious rather than resembling a conventional fish.
  • The external gills should be its defining visual feature.

Possible coloration:

  • Soft pink/coral base.
  • Blue or teal accents.
  • Pale markings around the face and gills.

Behaviour:

  • Curious and friendly.
  • Comfortable around both people and other creatures.
  • Loves water and frequently spends long periods swimming.
  • Can be somewhat timid when first encountering unfamiliar environments.
  • Becomes playful and energetic once comfortable.

Tide affinity manifestation:

  • Naturally excellent swimmer.
  • Can manipulate small amounts of surrounding water.
  • Can breathe comfortably underwater.
  • Uses its tail and body shape for efficient swimming.

Combat identity:

  • Beginner-friendly aquatic fighter.
  • Uses water-based attacks and evasive swimming.
  • More defensive and adaptable than aggressive.

Pirello

Average Height: .60 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

Species concept: Larger, more capable amphibious creature developing Frost characteristics. Appearance:

  • Longer and more streamlined body than Pipiri.
  • Stronger legs capable of supporting extended movement on land.
  • Larger external gills with more elaborate branching structures.
  • More prominent translucent tail fin.
  • Small aquatic crest extending along the back.
  • Slightly larger eyes and more mature facial structure.
  • More developed limbs while retaining the unmistakable axolotl-inspired anatomy.
  • Early Frost characteristics begin appearing around the gills and fins.
  • Tiny crystalline structures can form around the gills when its affinity is active.

Behaviour:

  • More confident and adventurous.
  • Equally comfortable exploring land and water.
  • Becomes fascinated by cold environments.
  • May deliberately seek cool streams, mountain pools and icy water.
  • More independent than Pipiri but retains its friendly temperament.

Tide affinity manifestation:

  • Greater water manipulation.
  • Strong swimming ability.
  • Can generate and shape streams of water.

Frost affinity manifestation:

  • Can lower the temperature of water it manipulates.
  • Begins producing small ice crystals.
  • Can create thin layers of frost around its body or nearby surfaces.
  • Frost initially manifests as a secondary enhancement to its Tide abilities.

Combat identity:

  • Flexible aquatic fighter.
  • Uses water for control and Frost to restrict movement.
  • Can switch between close-range amphibious movement and ranged water attacks.

Piravelle

Average Height: 1.00 m Average Length: 1.50 m Size Class: Large

Species concept: Elegant mature amphibious creature combining Tide and Frost. Appearance:

  • Long, graceful amphibious body.
  • Four strong but relatively short limbs.
  • Large flowing tail fin.
  • Elaborate branching external gills resembling frozen aquatic coral.
  • Crystalline structures integrated around the head and gills.
  • Smooth body contrasting with the harder crystalline structures.
  • More refined and elegant silhouette than the previous stages.
  • Deep blue/teal coloration with pale icy accents.
  • Gills release tiny crystalline particles when its Frost affinity is active.
  • The creature should still unmistakably resemble the same axolotl-inspired family as Pipiri.

Behaviour:

  • Calm, graceful and observant.
  • Prefers clean, quiet bodies of water.
  • Can become playful around trusted companions.
  • Often remains motionless for long periods while observing its surroundings.
  • Protective of aquatic habitats.

Tide affinity manifestation:

  • Powerful water manipulation.
  • Can control substantial quantities of water.
  • Exceptional underwater mobility.
  • Can create currents and manipulate the surrounding aquatic environment.

Frost affinity manifestation:

  • Can rapidly freeze portions of the water it controls.
  • Creates crystalline structures and ice formations.
  • Can lower surrounding water temperature dramatically.
  • Frost manifests naturally through its gills and aquatic structures.

Core conceptual relationship:

Tide controls water. Frost controls its temperature.

Rather than feeling like two unrelated powers, the two affinities should naturally complement one another. Combat identity:

  • Control-oriented aquatic fighter.
  • Uses Tide to manipulate positioning and the battlefield.
  • Uses Frost to restrict movement and create defensive structures.
  • Particularly powerful when fighting near water.

Evolution progression:

Pipiri: Cute aquatic amphibian → Pirello: Developing amphibious Tide/Frost creature → Piravelle: Elegant Tide/Frost aquatic creature

Starter Line 3 — Morrowe → Morvane → Morvayne

Affinity: Umbral → Umbral/Mystic → Umbral/Mystic Evolution: Three-stage Role: Nocturnal Umbral starter line

Morrowe

Average Height: .40 m Average Length: .75 m Size Class: Small

Species concept: Small dark-coated foxlike nocturnal creature. Appearance:

  • Small quadrupedal body.
  • Foxlike silhouette without being a direct fox copy.
  • Dark coat.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Pointed ears.
  • Soft, full tail.
  • Compact paws.
  • Subtle markings that become more visible in low light.
  • Overall appearance should be cute but mysterious.
  • Distinct from Nimblet by having a clearly quadrupedal, foxlike silhouette.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet and observant.
  • More cautious around strangers than Pipiri or Nimblet.
  • Highly curious but prefers watching from a safe distance.
  • Most active around dusk and nighttime.
  • Forms strong bonds with trusted companions.
  • Can be mischievous when comfortable.

Umbral affinity manifestation:

  • Naturally comfortable in darkness.
  • Can move quietly.
  • Has strong low-light vision.
  • Can obscure its presence in dim environments.

Combat identity:

  • Fast, evasive and stealth-oriented.
  • Uses darkness and positioning.
  • Relies on surprise attacks rather than brute strength.

Morvane

Average Height: .80 m Average Length: 1.30 m Size Class: Medium

Species concept: Larger, more mature nocturnal foxlike creature beginning to develop Mystic characteristics. Appearance:

  • Taller and leaner than Morrowe.
  • Longer legs.
  • Larger, fuller tail.
  • More pronounced ears.
  • Dark coat with subtle mystical markings.
  • Eyes become more luminous.
  • Small mystical wisps or faint energy patterns can appear around the tail and paws.
  • Retains the recognizable quadrupedal foxlike anatomy of Morrowe.
  • Silhouette becomes more elegant and predatory.

Behaviour:

  • More independent and confident.
  • Highly observant.
  • Prefers quiet environments.
  • Can remain completely still for long periods while watching its surroundings.
  • Develops a stronger affinity for moonlit or secluded places.

Umbral affinity manifestation:

  • Greater stealth.
  • Can blend into shadows.
  • Can suppress its presence for short periods.
  • Excellent nighttime hunter.

Mystic affinity manifestation:

  • Can sense unusual energy.
  • Can create faint illusion-like distortions.
  • Begins manipulating subtle mystical energy rather than relying purely on physical abilities.

Combat identity:

  • Stealth/control fighter.
  • Combines Umbral concealment with Mystic tricks.
  • Uses deception and misdirection to create openings.

Morvayne

Average Height: 1.15 m Average Length: 1.90 m Size Class: Large

Species concept: Elegant mature nocturnal creature combining Umbral stealth and Mystic energy. Appearance:

  • Sleek, graceful quadrupedal body.
  • Long limbs and refined proportions.
  • Large, expressive ears.
  • Long, luxurious tail.
  • Dark coat with subtle luminous/mystical markings.
  • Eyes glow more strongly when using its Mystic affinity.
  • Wisps of mystical energy can trail from its tail and paws.
  • Markings may resemble faint constellations, moonlit patterns or abstract mystical symbols.
  • Retains enough of Morrowe’s anatomy to clearly read as the final stage of the same family.
  • Should look mysterious and elegant rather than monstrous.

Behaviour:

  • Calm, intelligent and highly perceptive.
  • Extremely loyal to trusted companions.
  • Comfortable operating alone.
  • Often observes situations before deciding how to act.
  • Has an almost uncanny ability to notice things others miss.
  • Becomes fiercely protective when its companions are threatened.

Umbral affinity manifestation:

  • Exceptional stealth.
  • Can move almost silently.
  • Can blend into darkness.
  • Can temporarily obscure itself from casual observation.

Mystic affinity manifestation:

  • More sophisticated energy manipulation.
  • Can create illusions or distort perceptions.
  • Can sense unusual magical/mystical phenomena.
  • Can manipulate mystical energy around itself.

Combat identity:

  • Fast, intelligent control fighter.
  • Combines stealth, misdirection and mystical effects.
  • Wins through positioning and deception rather than raw power.

Evolution progression:

Morrowe: Cute mysterious nocturnal foxling → Morvane: Elegant developing Umbral/Mystic creature → Morvayne: Refined Umbral/Mystic nocturnal hunter

10–12 — Kivvi → Kivara → Kivarune

Affinity: Radiant → Radiant/Frost → Radiant/Frost Rarity: Common → Common → Uncommon Core concept: Ethereal light creature

Kivvi

Average Height: .25 m Average Length: .35 m Size Class: Tiny

  • Very small, legless, floating creature.
  • Soft, rounded central body, almost like a little wisp or spirit.
  • No conventional limbs; it naturally hovers a short distance above the ground.
  • Small ear-like or fin-like extensions around its head.
  • Large, expressive eyes.
  • A tiny luminous crystal/orb embedded in its forehead or chest.
  • Lower body gradually fades into a faint translucent glow rather than ending in feet or a tail.
  • Subtle particles of light drift around it when it moves.
  • Its movement should feel effortless—floating, bobbing and gently drifting rather than flying.
  • Mostly pale/soft coloration with warm Radiant highlights.
  • Cute and ethereal, not ghostly or spooky.

Behaviour:

  • Curious, playful and easily distracted by light.
  • Likes floating around people and following reflections.
  • Can drift through small gaps that ordinary creatures cannot comfortably navigate.
  • Often settles above flowers, water or other reflective surfaces.
  • Naturally drawn toward sunlight.

Radiant affinity:

  • Emits a gentle glow.
  • Can create little floating motes of light.
  • Produces brief flashes when startled.
  • The forehead/chest crystal focuses its Radiant energy.

Combat identity: Small, evasive floating creature using light flashes and ranged Radiant attacks.

Kivara

Average Height: .45 m Average Length: .65 m Size Class: Small

The evolution should retain the floating/ethereal anatomy rather than suddenly growing legs.

  • Larger, more defined floating body.
  • More pronounced luminous crystal.
  • Ethereal lower body becomes longer and more flowing.
  • Several translucent, ribbon-like energy structures trail behind it.
  • Pale blue-white Frost crystals begin forming naturally around the body.
  • Radiant markings become more elaborate.
  • The creature appears to be simultaneously emitting warmth/light and cold crystalline energy.

Kivarune

Average Height: .75 m Average Length: 1.05 m Size Class: Medium

  • Elegant, larger floating creature.
  • No legs.
  • Long, flowing ethereal lower body.
  • Several crystalline structures orbit or naturally protrude from its body.
  • Large luminous central crystal.
  • Frost crystals become more prominent but remain integrated with its Radiant appearance.
  • Its translucent body allows Radiant light to shine through it.
  • When moving, it leaves behind a faint trail of sparkling light and tiny ice crystals.
  • Should feel mystical and almost celestial, while still clearly being a living creature rather than a ghost.

This gives Kivvi’s line a very distinct identity: one of our genuinely strange creatures rather than “cute animal with elemental powers.”

13–15 — Brindlew → Brindrel → Brinderv

Affinity: Verdant → Verdant/Stone → Verdant/Stone Rarity: Common → Common → Uncommon Core concept: Bidoof × Nuzleaf-inspired bipedal woodland creature

Brindlew

Average Height: .50 m Average Length: .80 m Size Class: Small

  • Small bipedal woodland creature.
  • Stocky body with short but strong legs.
  • Large, prominent front teeth.
  • Rounded cheeks and slightly buck-toothed face.
  • Short arms with small hands/claws.
  • Large leafy structures around its head/ears, giving it a Nuzleaf-like silhouette.
  • Thick little tail.
  • Brown/tan body with green leafy accents.
  • Slightly oversized feet.
  • Should look adorably awkward, rather than sleek.
  • The leafy structures are part of its natural anatomy rather than something it wears.
  • Small patches of moss or tiny leaves can grow naturally along its back.

Behaviour:

  • Curious, energetic and somewhat mischievous.
  • Loves chewing wood, roots and hard seeds.
  • Excellent digger.
  • Frequently gathers nuts, seeds and interesting stones.
  • Can be surprisingly stubborn.
  • Often stands upright while investigating something.

Verdant affinity:

  • Can encourage small plants to grow.
  • Can manipulate nearby grass, roots and small vines.
  • Seeds it carries can sprout unusually quickly.

Combat identity: A scrappy little physical fighter with basic Verdant abilities.

Brindrel

Average Height: .95 m Average Length: 1.40 m Size Class: Medium

  • Taller, sturdier bipedal body.
  • Stronger arms and larger hands.
  • More pronounced teeth.
  • Leaf structures around the head become larger and more elaborate.
  • A leafy mane or collar begins forming around its shoulders.
  • Small natural stone growths begin appearing on its forearms, shoulders and feet.
  • Tail becomes thicker and more branch-like.
  • Body remains brown/earthy with increasingly vibrant green vegetation.
  • More upright posture.

Verdant: stronger vines, roots and plant growth. Stone: develops the ability to harden portions of its body and manipulate small stones. Combat identity: Physical bruiser with plant-based control and increasingly strong Stone defence.

Brinderv

Average Height: 1.55 m Average Length: 2.10 m Size Class: Very Large

  • Large, muscular bipedal woodland creature.
  • Powerful legs and broad shoulders.
  • Strong arms with large clawed hands.
  • Large leafy structures framing the head.
  • Thick leafy mane around neck and shoulders.
  • Mature vegetation grows naturally across portions of its body.
  • Stone plates have developed across its shoulders, forearms, lower legs and back.
  • Some stone formations resemble tree roots or naturally weathered rock.
  • Thick tail resembles a sturdy branch covered in leaves.
  • Large front teeth remain as a visual connection to Brindlew.
  • Should look like the final evolution of that goofy little woodland rodent, not a completely unrelated forest monster.

Behaviour:

  • Protective of its territory.
  • Still enjoys gathering and collecting things.
  • Extremely strong physically.
  • Uses its strength to move fallen trees and large rocks.
  • Often acts as a protector for smaller woodland creatures.

Combat identity: Heavy physical Verdant/Stone fighter.

16–18 — Sovel → Sovelle → Sovaryn

Affinity: Gale → Gale/Radiant → Gale/Radiant Rarity: Common → Common → Uncommon Core concept: Serpentine wind creature

Sovel

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Small

  • Small serpentine creature.
  • No legs.
  • Long, slender body.
  • Smooth scales.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Small head with subtle horn/fin-like structures.
  • Short tapering tail.
  • Body naturally curves into floating loops while moving.
  • Instead of simply slithering everywhere, Sovel can use Gale energy to lift and propel itself through the air.
  • Small fins, membranes or feather-like structures could run along portions of its body, but nothing resembling conventional wings.
  • Gale energy constantly creates tiny currents around its body.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely playful and energetic.
  • Loves winding around objects and chasing moving things.
  • Frequently launches itself into the air and rides its own wind currents.
  • Enjoys racing other creatures.
  • Curious and somewhat mischievous.

Gale affinity:

  • Generates small gusts.
  • Uses wind to propel itself.
  • Can briefly hover.
  • Can rapidly change direction in midair.

Combat identity: Fast, evasive serpentine attacker that uses momentum and wind.

Sovelle

Average Height: .45 m Average Length: 1.60 m Size Class: Medium

  • Longer and more elegant body.
  • More pronounced head crest.
  • Fins/membranes along the body become larger.
  • Can remain airborne for considerably longer.
  • Gale energy visibly flows around its body while moving.
  • Small luminous markings begin appearing along its scales.
  • Radiant energy manifests primarily around the head and along the body.

Gale: Much greater aerial mobility and wind manipulation. Radiant: Can produce flashes and concentrated bursts of light. Combat identity: Highly mobile aerial skirmisher.

Sovaryn

Average Height: .65 m Average Length: 3.20 m Size Class: Large

  • Long, powerful serpentine body.
  • No legs.
  • Large elegant head.
  • Pronounced crest/horn structures.
  • Multiple flowing aerodynamic fins or membrane-like structures along the body.
  • Scales become sleek and refined.
  • Luminous Radiant markings run along the length of its body.
  • Gale energy continuously distorts the air around it.
  • When flying, its body forms enormous flowing arcs through the air rather than flapping wings.
  • Radiant energy can illuminate its entire body.
  • Long trailing tail.
    • May have a subtle sky-serpent aesthetic without resembling a conventional dragon.

Behaviour:

  • Graceful and highly intelligent.
  • Enjoys flying through storms and strong winds.
  • Often circles high above its territory.
  • Curious about distant locations.
  • More contemplative than the energetic Sovel.

Gale affinity:

  • Exceptional aerial mobility.
  • Can manipulate surrounding air currents.
  • Can create powerful gusts and localized wind phenomena.
  • Can accelerate to tremendous speeds.

Radiant affinity:

  • Creates powerful flashes.
  • Can generate concentrated beams or bursts of light.
  • Luminous markings become extremely bright when its power is active.

Combat identity: Fast, graceful aerial attacker that essentially swims through the sky on currents of wind, using Radiant attacks from unexpected angles.

19–21 — Tarnit → Tarnelle → Tarnovar

Affinity: Stone → Stone/Metal → Stone/Metal Rarity: Common → Common → Uncommon Species concept: Tiny rocky baby rhino → armored rhino-like powerhouse

Tarnit

Average Height: .55 m Average Length: .85 m Size Class: Small

  • Tiny baby rhinoceros-like creature, quadrupedal.
  • Very compact, rounded body with short, sturdy legs.
  • Large head relative to its body, giving it a particularly cute silhouette.
  • Small, rounded stone horn on its nose.
  • Thick rocky hide with smooth, rounded mineral plates rather than sharp armor.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Small ears that can perk up or fold slightly.
  • Stubby little tail.
  • Feet look almost like rounded chunks of stone.
  • Mostly earthy gray/brown coloration with subtle mineral variation.
  • A few tiny pebbly formations along its back.
  • Despite being made partly of rock, it should look soft and baby-like, not like a miniature tank.

Behaviour:

  • Curious and stubborn.
  • Likes bumping its little horn against rocks.
  • Will happily follow larger creatures around.
  • When frightened, instinctively curls its body and hardens its rocky hide.

Tarnelle

Average Height: 1.00 m Average Length: 1.65 m Size Class: Large

  • Significantly larger but still clearly rhino-like.
  • Body becomes stockier and more muscular.
  • Horn grows longer and develops a crystalline texture.
  • Stone plates become larger across the shoulders, back and sides.
  • Metallic veins begin appearing through the stone.
  • Legs become powerful and heavily grounded.
  • Head retains the broad, recognizable Tarnit shape.
  • Small metal plates begin forming around the horn and forehead.

This is where the line transitions from cute baby rock-rhino into something that looks genuinely formidable.

Tarnovar

Average Height: 1.70 m Average Length: 2.90 m Size Class: Very Large

  • Large, powerful rocky rhinoceros-like creature.
  • Massive shoulders and thick legs.
  • Large reinforced horn combining stone and metal.
  • Heavy stone armor covering much of the body.
  • Metallic plates have developed between the natural stone formations.
  • Broad armored head.
  • Thick neck and powerful charging stance.
  • Stone and metal should look like they grew together naturally, rather than conventional manufactured armor.
  • Still retains Tarnit’s basic rhino silhouette and facial structure.

Evolution progression: Baby rhino → young armored rhino → massive living stone/metal rhino.

22–24 — Quiblet → Quivane → Quivaryn

Affinity: Flame → Flame/Volt → Flame/Volt Rarity: Common → Common → Uncommon Species concept: Cute avian flame creature → powerful elemental bird

Quiblet

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .50 m Size Class: Small

  • Small avian creature, roughly the size of a young bird of prey.
  • Round, fluffy body.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Short beak.
  • Small wings that are slightly oversized for its body.
  • Tiny talons.
  • Flame-like feathers around its head and tail.
  • Its tail consists of a few feathers with tiny flames burning at their tips.
  • Warm reds, oranges and golden tones.
  • Wings have subtle flame-shaped feather patterns.
  • Should look adorable and energetic, rather than like a serious fire bird.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely energetic.
  • Likes hopping, fluttering and climbing rather than flying long distances.
  • Frequently puffs up its feathers when excited.
  • Its flames become brighter when it is happy or agitated.
  • Occasionally singes things accidentally.

Quivane

Average Height: .70 m Average Length: 1.10 m Size Class: Medium

  • Larger and more streamlined.
  • Wings become substantially larger.
  • Flame-like feathers extend from the wings and tail.
  • Crest develops on the head.
  • Small electrical sparks begin appearing between its feathers.
  • Some feathers develop darkened tips that occasionally crackle with Volt energy.
  • More capable of sustained flight.
  • Its movements become fast and erratic.

Flame: Primarily concentrated through wings, tail and crest. Volt: Initially manifests as sparks and electrical discharges around the feathers.

Quivaryn

Average Height: 1.25 m Average Length: 2.00 m Size Class: Large

  • Majestic but still recognizably descended from the tiny Quiblet.
  • Large wings with long, elegant flight feathers.
  • Flame burns naturally along the edges of its wings and tail.
  • Pronounced feathered crest.
  • Electrical energy runs visibly through portions of its plumage.
  • Tail feathers become long and dramatic, combining Flame and Volt effects.
  • Powerful talons.
  • Streamlined body designed for aerial speed.
  • Should look like a storm-fire bird, not simply a flaming eagle.

Combat identity: Extremely mobile aerial Flame/Volt attacker, combining fiery dives, electrical discharges and high-speed flight.

25–27 — Elnu → Elvara → Elvarin

Affinity: Mystic → Mystic/Radiant → Mystic/Radiant Rarity: Common → Common → Rare Species concept: Cute little priestess → elegant shrine maiden → refined mystical guardian

Elnu

Average Height: .40 m Average Length: .35 m Size Class: Small

  • Small bipedal humanoid creature.
  • Childlike proportions: short body, slightly oversized head.
  • Very large, expressive eyes.
  • Small delicate hands and feet.
  • Long ears or subtle mystical extensions around the sides of the head.
  • Wears a simple, naturally grown/fitted garment resembling a tiny ceremonial robe.
  • Clothing should feel like part of its species’ appearance rather than literal human clothing.
  • Small decorative ribbons or talisman-like markings.
  • A tiny mystical charm/crystal hangs near its neck or forehead.
  • Soft, pale coloration with subtle mystical markings.
  • Gentle, innocent facial expression.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet and observant.
  • Naturally drawn to places of spiritual or mystical significance.
  • Often sits quietly with its hands together as though meditating.
  • Curious about people but initially shy.
  • Its large eyes make its expressions particularly obvious.

Mystic affinity:

  • Creates tiny illusions.
  • Can sense unusual energies.
  • Produces faint mystical lights.
  • Can momentarily make small objects appear somewhere they aren’t.

Elvara

Average Height: .80 m Average Length: .65 m Size Class: Medium

  • Taller and more slender while retaining somewhat cute proportions.
  • Large eyes remain, but facial features become more mature and elegant.
  • Long hair-like appendages or naturally flowing strands develop around the head.
  • Garment evolves into a more recognizable shrine-maiden-inspired ceremonial outfit.
  • Long sleeves and flowing fabric-like structures create an elegant silhouette.
  • Decorative ribbons/talismans become more prominent.
  • Mystical symbols appear across the outfit and body.
  • A Radiant glow begins appearing around her hands and ceremonial ornaments.
  • Small floating charms or luminous symbols may accompany her Mystic abilities.

The visual progression should feel like: tiny temple child → young shrine maiden.

Elvarin

Average Height: 1.58 m Average Length: .95 m Size Class: Large

  • Elegant, refined humanoid mystical creature.
  • Slender but graceful physique.
  • Long flowing hair/appendages.
  • Large eyes remain as a recognizable species trait, though more mature in expression.
  • Elaborate shrine-maiden-inspired ceremonial attire.
  • Long flowing sleeves and layered garments.
  • Multiple ornamental charms/talismans.
  • A luminous central charm, crystal or sigil.
  • Mystic symbols appear naturally throughout her design.
  • Radiant energy manifests as elegant halos, floating symbols and soft luminous patterns.
  • When using powerful Mystic abilities, several talisman-like constructs can orbit around her.
  • Should feel serene, beautiful and spiritually powerful, rather than overtly combat-oriented.

Behaviour:

  • Calm and composed.
  • Protective toward smaller creatures.
  • Appears to understand mystical phenomena instinctively.
  • Often acts as a mediator rather than an aggressor.
  • Has a strong connection to ancient places, shrines and natural spiritual sites.

Combat identity: A Rare Mystic/Radiant creature specializing in illusions, barriers, purification/light techniques and mystical constructs.

28–30 — Drakyn → Voltaryn → Drakoryn

Affinity: Dragon → Dragon/Volt → Dragon/Volt
Rarity: Rare → Extremely Rare → Extremely Rare
Evolution: Three-stage Core concept: Rare quadrupedal dragon that develops into an imposing bipedal Dragon/Volt powerhouse

028 — Drakyn

Average Height: .55 m Average Length: .95 m Size Class: Small

Species concept: Small quadrupedal young dragon.

Physical Description:

  • Small, four-legged dragon with a compact, slightly rounded body.
  • Large head relative to its body, giving it a youthful but distinctly draconic appearance.
  • Short muzzle with small visible fangs.
  • Small horns beginning to develop.
  • Four sturdy legs ending in small claws.
  • Short developing wings that are not yet capable of sustained flight.
  • Long tail, slightly oversized for its body.
  • Distinctive scale pattern that can develop into the mature markings of the later stages.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Subtle electrical phenomena may occasionally appear around the horns or tail, foreshadowing the Volt affinity that develops later.
  • The overall silhouette should immediately read as a rare young dragon rather than a generic reptile with wings.

Personality:

  • Curious, energetic and highly expressive.
  • Proud of its developing horns and wings despite its small size.
  • Playful with trusted companions but quick to react when challenged.
  • Displays flashes of the confidence and intensity that will define its mature form.

Affinity manifestation:

  • Dragon energy is naturally present throughout its body.
  • Volt has not formally manifested yet, although small electrical discharges may appear instinctively during excitement or stress.

Combat identity:

  • Physically capable for its size.
  • Favors claws, bites, tail strikes and short bursts of draconic energy.
  • Agile rather than overwhelmingly powerful.

029 — Voltaryn

Average Height: 1.35 m Average Length: 2.20 m Size Class: Large

Species concept: Adolescent quadrupedal dragon whose dormant Volt affinity has awakened.

Physical Description:

  • Significantly larger and more muscular than Drakyn.
  • Remains fully quadrupedal.
  • Head becomes more angular and mature while retaining the recognizable features of Drakyn.
  • Horns grow considerably and become important conduits for electrical energy.
  • Wings become large enough for sustained flight.
  • Legs become powerful and heavily clawed.
  • Tail becomes thicker and longer.
  • Dragon scales develop stronger, more distinctive patterning.
  • Some scales develop subtle conductive ridges that channel electrical energy through the body.
  • Eyes become sharper and more intense.
  • Electrical energy naturally travels through the horns, wing structures and tail when Volt abilities are active.

Personality:

  • More confident, competitive and independent than Drakyn.
  • Enjoys testing its growing strength and aerial ability.
  • Can become impatient with creatures it considers weak or overly cautious.
  • Forms powerful bonds with trusted companions and becomes fiercely protective of them.

Dragon affinity manifestation:

  • Physical strength increases dramatically.
  • Draconic energy becomes denser and more destructive.
  • Its claws, jaws and tail become important weapons rather than merely defensive features.

Volt affinity manifestation:

  • Volt fully awakens during this stage.
  • Electricity can travel naturally through the horns, wing structures, conductive scales and tail.
  • Can discharge electrical energy through physical attacks.
  • Flight can be accompanied by crackling electrical energy around the wings.

Combat identity:

  • Powerful mobile physical attacker.
  • Combines aerial mobility with Dragon and Volt attacks.
  • Uses electricity to amplify claws, bites, charges and other physical strikes.
  • More dangerous at close range than its ranged abilities alone might suggest.

030 — Drakoryn

Average Height: 2.40 m Average Length: 3.50 m Size Class: Huge

Species concept: Imposing bipedal Dragon/Volt apex creature.

Physical Description:

  • Large, imposing bipedal dragon.
  • Powerful hind legs support a dramatically larger body while remaining unmistakably draconic.
  • Massive torso and broad shoulders.
  • Large, muscular arms ending in powerful claws.
  • Huge wings extending from the upper back.
  • Long, muscular tail used for balance and combat.
  • Large, intimidating head with prominent horns.
  • Strong jaw and visible fangs.
  • Heavy mature scales form natural armor over vulnerable areas.
  • The transition from quadrupedal to bipedal is obvious: the forelegs have developed into powerful arms while the hind legs support the creature’s mature frame.
  • Dragon/Volt energy is fully integrated into its anatomy.
  • Electrical channels run along the horns, shoulders, wing structures and tail.
  • When charging an attack, electricity visibly travels through these structures before being released.
  • Wing edges may crackle with electricity when fully energized.
  • Powerful claws and physical attacks remain as important as its elemental abilities.

Personality:

  • Confident, formidable and highly self-assured.
  • Possesses a strong protective instinct toward trusted companions and its territory.
  • Rarely needs to demonstrate its strength without reason, but responds decisively to serious threats.
  • Retains recognizable traces of Drakyn’s curiosity and Voltaryn’s competitive nature beneath its mature presence.

Dragon affinity manifestation:

  • Exceptional physical strength and durability.
  • Dense draconic energy reinforces its body and attacks.
  • Can combine powerful physical strikes with concentrated Dragon energy.

Volt affinity manifestation:

  • Electricity is integrated throughout the creature’s mature anatomy.
  • Can channel electrical energy through its claws, horns, wings and tail.
  • Can release concentrated electrical attacks after charging its internal conductive channels.
  • Electrical output becomes visibly stronger when Drakoryn is preparing a major attack.

Combat identity:

  • High-power physical attacker with strong Dragon/Volt coverage.
  • Combines immense physical force with explosive electrical attacks.
  • Uses its wings for mobility rather than relying solely on ranged attacks.
  • Can fight effectively at close range while retaining powerful ranged options.

Evolution progression:

Drakyn: Cute quadrupedal young dragon → Voltaryn: Powerful flying quadrupedal Dragon/Volt adolescent → Drakoryn: Imposing bipedal Dragon/Volt powerhouse

Visual identity:

Drakoryn should create an immediate sense of scale and power while remaining unmistakably its own species. Its silhouette, horn structure, wing design, markings, coloration and anatomy should be distinct from existing dragon designs. The final form should feel like the culmination of the entire evolutionary line rather than a completely different creature.

31–32 — Vessa → Vessari

Affinity: Tide → Tide/Dragon Rarity: Common → Rare

Vessa

Average Height: .35 m Average Length: .80 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny elegant aquatic creature with Milotic-like potential

  • Small elegant fins around its head.
  • Smooth scales with subtle iridescence.
  • Long flowing tail ending in a graceful fin.
  • Small dorsal fin.
  • Delicate facial features.
  • Primarily aquatic coloration, but with subtle decorative patterns.
  • Should look cute and graceful rather than goofy.
  • Even at this stage, there should be hints that it could eventually become something remarkably beautiful.

Behaviour:

  • Gentle and curious.
  • Loves swimming around people.
  • Particularly attracted to clean, clear water.
  • Can become surprisingly playful despite its elegant appearance.

Vessari

Average Height: .85 m Average Length: 2.10 m Size Class: Large

  • Tide energy flows around its body while swimming.
  • Could create beautiful spiralling water currents around itself.

Aesthetic: Elegant, almost mythical aquatic beauty with just enough Dragon influence to make its evolution feel special. So rather than: it’s:

tiny graceful aquatic creature → majestic serpentine Tide/Dragon.

Much more cohesive.

  • Small aviator-style goggles can form part of its distinctive visual identity—perhaps worn naturally over the eyes as part of its species’ gear-like anatomy, or as a culturally characteristic accessory.
  • The goggles should feel like an intentional part of the creature’s design rather than random human clothing.
  • Could have small metallic harness-like structures around the head/neck that complement them.

Overall concept: And importantly, the goggles give Rookane an immediately recognizable silhouette without making the entire species “a robot bird.”

39–40 — Pellin → Pellune

Affinity: Frost → Frost/Mystic Rarity: Common → Uncommon

Average Height: .35 m Average Length: .65 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny snowy penguin-like creature

  • Tiny Frost crystals can form along the edges of its feathers after spending time in cold weather.
  • Small webbed feet adapted for sliding across ice.
  • Short tail feathers.
  • Its body should remain cute and compact, almost like a little waddling snowball.

Behaviour:

  • Produces small bursts of freezing air.

040 — Pellune

Average Height: .80 m Average Length: 1.10 m Size Class: Medium

  • Becomes taller and more elegant while retaining the basic penguin-like anatomy.

  • Eyes become noticeably larger and more luminous.
  • Several crystalline structures develop around the head and shoulders.
  • These crystals are not simply ice—they act as Mystic energy conduits.
  • Frost remains present throughout its body, but the visual emphasis shifts toward ethereal blue/purple mystical energy.
  • Objects around it can subtly float or shift when it concentrates.
  • Small snowflakes and glowing particles may orbit around it when using its powers.
  • Its movements become unusually graceful, almost as though gravity affects it slightly less than other creatures.

Mystic abilities:

  • Telekinetic manipulation of small objects.
  • Psychic barriers.
  • Illusions.
  • Can influence the movement of surrounding snow and ice without physically touching them.

Evolution progression:

Cute snowy penguin → elegant psychic/mystical penguin creature

41–42 — Arko → Arkelle

  • It gently bobs up and down while hovering.

Behaviour:

  • Follows people around like a little floating lantern.
  • Likes warm places.
  • Gets excited when someone makes a campfire.
  • When frightened, its flame shrinks dramatically.
  • When angry, the flame shoots upward like a miniature torch.

Flame affinity:

  • Produces small fireballs.
  • Can increase or decrease the intensity of its central flame.
  • Can briefly surround itself with a small ring of fire.

Average Height: .80 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

  • Several smaller flames may burn around its metallic frame.
  • It can hover much higher and move considerably faster.
  • Metal components can detach and move independently under its control.
  • Could create temporary metallic shields around its central flame.
  • Decorative structures might resemble the architecture of an ancient ceremonial brazier or floating shrine.

Flame: powerful fire projection and heat manipulation. Metal: control of its metallic components, defensive barriers and metallic projectiles. Overall concept:

Cute floating fire bowl → majestic floating ceremonial brazier

044 — Sairune

Average Height: 1.00 m at the shoulder Average Length: 1.90 m Size Class: Large

Concept: imposing direwolf-like mystical wind wolf

  • Powerful shoulders and heavily muscled legs give it a formidable stance.
  • Large paws and strong claws reinforce its role as a powerful terrestrial predator.
  • Head becomes broader and more wolf-like, with a strong muzzle and pronounced jaw.
  • Ears remain pointed but are proportionally smaller than Sairi’s, contributing to the heavier silhouette.
  • Long, thick tail rather than a delicate ribbon-like plume.
  • Fur remains sleek enough to preserve its wind affinity, but the overall body is dense and powerful rather than elegant.
  • Mystic markings develop across the face, shoulders and flanks, glowing softly when its power is active.
  • Wind naturally gathers around its mane and paws, especially when it prepares to charge.
  • Its movement remains remarkably fast for its size, but it now combines speed with considerable physical force.
  • When fully energized, swirling translucent wind and Mystic particles surround its body like a moving storm.

Mystic abilities:

  • Creates illusions through distorted air and light.
  • Can briefly become difficult to perceive while moving.
  • Can sense unusual energies.
  • Creates mystical wind barriers.

Evolution progression:

Young wolf pup with a supernatural breeze → elegant Gale/Mystic wolf

57–58 — Koroa → Korovan

057 — Koroa
