  • Primarily ocean-blue, pale pink, cream and muted orange tones.
  • Small bubbles occasionally emerge from the coral formations.
  • It can survive both underwater and for limited periods on land, provided its body remains moist.

Behaviour:

  • Gentle and social.
  • Likes shallow reefs and warm coastal waters.
  • Often gathers in groups around larger coral formations.
  • Its coral growth slowly changes depending on the environment it lives in.

Tide affinity:

  • Manipulates small amounts of water.
  • Creates bubbles and small currents.
  • Can draw moisture toward itself to maintain its coral growth.

058 — Korovan

Average Height: 1.10 m Average Length: 1.80 m Size Class: Large

The Stone affinity can now represent reef structures becoming massive mineralized coral.

  • Larger and substantially more robust.
  • Coral growths become extensive, branching across its back and sides.
  • Parts of the coral have hardened into stone-like mineral structures.
  • Several large formations resemble natural reef outcroppings.
  • Its body becomes partially armored by mineralized coral.
  • Tide energy flows through channels between the coral structures.
  • Can grow temporary coral barriers from the ground or seabed.
  • Can manipulate surrounding water to create currents around its body.
  • More defensive than aggressive.

Evolution progression:

Tiny living coral creature → imposing mobile coral-reef guardian.

And the name Koroa → Korovan suddenly feels even more intentional. 😄

59–60 — Esvin → Esvar

Affinity: Umbral → Umbral/Metal

059 — Esvin

Average Height: .35 m Average Length: .50 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny ghostly creature

  • Small, floating spectral creature rather than a conventional animal.
  • Its lower body gradually fades into a wispy, translucent tail instead of having legs.
  • Rounded upper body with small floating appendages.
  • Large, expressive eyes.
  • Slightly oversized head.
  • Small wisps of spectral energy constantly drift from its body.
  • Its form is somewhat translucent, allowing vague shapes behind it to be seen through its edges.
  • Pale gray, blue-gray and desaturated violet tones.
  • A small luminous core sits somewhere within its chest.
  • Its expression should be cute and curious rather than frightening.
  • When startled, its entire body briefly becomes almost invisible.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet and inquisitive.
  • Fascinated by old objects, abandoned buildings and places with strong emotional history.
  • Likes floating behind people without making a sound.
  • Can become attached to people it trusts.
  • Sometimes accidentally scares people simply by forgetting that they cannot hear it approach.

Umbral affinity:

  • Can phase partially through solid objects.
  • Can fade into darkness.
  • Can become temporarily intangible.
  • Can sense other sources of Umbral energy.

060 — Esvar

Average Height: 1.20 m Average Length: 1.60 m Size Class: Large

  • Larger spectral body with a much more defined silhouette.
  • Wispy lower body becomes longer and more elaborate.
  • Spectral energy forms a flowing cloak-like shape around its body.
  • A dark metallic ornamental frame develops around its chest and shoulders.
  • Metal does not cover its entire body; instead, it appears almost like ancient ceremonial armor suspended around a ghost.
  • Metallic fragments float independently around it.
  • Its luminous core becomes brighter and more visible.
  • Face becomes more mature and imposing while retaining recognizable features from Esvin.
  • Long spectral appendages can form temporarily during combat.
  • The floating metal pieces can rearrange themselves into shields, blades or defensive structures.
  • Its body can partially phase through the metal formations, giving it a very unusual silhouette.

Umbral: phasing, concealment, spectral movement and intangible attacks. Metal: manipulation of its floating metallic components and creation of defensive/offensive constructs. Evolution progression:

Cute wandering ghost → elegant armored spectral entity.

61–62 — Marnel → Marnyx

Tide/Wild → Tide/Wild

061 — Marnel

Average Height: .65 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

  • Small, chubby body with four short sturdy legs.
  • Rounded bear-like face and tiny rounded ears.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Short muzzle and little nose.
  • Dense, plush-looking fur with a naturally water-resistant texture.
  • Broad paws with subtle webbing between the toes.
  • Short, thick tail.
  • A slightly flattened body shape that makes it surprisingly buoyant.
  • Soft blue, gray, cream or sandy coloration.
  • Small fin-like ridges along its back that help stabilize it underwater.
  • Its paws and belly can develop faint Tide markings when submerged.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely curious and food-motivated.
  • Loves floating on its back.
  • Surprisingly capable swimmer despite its stubby proportions.
  • Uses its paws to dig through riverbeds and coastal shallows.
  • Often naps floating peacefully in calm water.

Wild: strong body, natural survival instincts and surprisingly powerful digging. Tide: water manipulation and aquatic mobility.

062 — Marnyx

Average Height: 1.55 m at the shoulder Average Length: 2.40 m Size Class: Very Large

  • Evolves into a much larger, powerfully built quadrupedal bear-like creature.
  • Its 1.55 m height is measured at the shoulder; standing upright on its hind legs, it would tower substantially over a human.
  • Still unmistakably derived from Marnel’s body shape.
  • Thick shoulders and powerful forelimbs.
  • Webbing becomes more pronounced.
  • Fur becomes sleek and dense rather than fluffy.
  • Small fin-like structures develop along its limbs and back.
  • Its paws can generate powerful currents when striking through water.
  • Tide markings become prominent across its body.
  • Large claws remain useful both on land and underwater.
  • Broad muzzle and stronger jaw give it a formidable appearance without turning it into a monster.

Overall progression:

Cute floating water bear → powerful amphibious bear-like guardian.

63–64 — Yori → Yorvale

Dragon → Dragon/Radiant The evolutionary progression reverses the posture: bipedal raptor-inspired dragon → powerful quadrupedal dragon.

063 — Yori

Average Height: .60 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

Concept: cute bipedal raptor-dragon

  • Small bipedal dragon with clear raptor-inspired anatomy.
  • Powerful digitigrade hind legs.
  • Small forearms with three clawed digits.
  • Long balancing tail.
  • Large head relative to its body.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Short muzzle with a small hooked dragon-like beak.
  • Tiny horn nubs rather than large horns.
  • Small ridges running from the back of its head down its neck.
  • Covered in small scales with a few softer feather-like structures around the neck and tail.
  • No wings.
  • Feet are large and clawed, giving it a slightly comical “baby predator” appearance.
  • Energetic, curious and prone to standing completely still before suddenly sprinting away.

The raptor influence should be visible in the legs, posture, tail and head, while the scales, horns and draconic facial features establish that it’s definitely a Dragon-affinity creature. Behaviour:

  • Curious and mischievous.
  • Likes chasing moving objects.
  • Attempts intimidating displays that are adorable rather than frightening.
  • Very fast over short distances.

064 — Yorvale

Average Height: 1.50 m Average Length: 2.60 m Size Class: Very Large

Yorvale represents a substantial transformation rather than simply an enlarged version of Yori.

  • Much larger, powerful quadrupedal dragon.
  • Evolution causes its body to shift from upright bipedal posture into a low, powerful four-legged stance.
  • Long muscular forelimbs replace Yori’s tiny arms.
  • Hind legs retain the raptor influence but become substantially stronger.
  • Long, sweeping tail used for balance and powerful directional movement.
  • Head becomes more elongated and regal.
  • Horns develop into elegant backward-sweeping structures.
  • No wings whatsoever.
  • No need for flight—the creature should feel like an ancient terrestrial dragon.
  • Scales become smooth and highly refined, with subtle iridescence.
  • Radiant energy appears as luminous markings between the scales.
  • A brilliant crest develops around the head and neck.
  • When it moves, faint motes of light follow its body.
  • Its eyes become intensely luminous.
  • The Radiant energy can form a subtle halo around its silhouette when it uses powerful attacks.

Dragon: immense physical power, draconic energy and natural resilience. Radiant: luminous energy, radiant breath/beam attacks and protective energy. Overall progression:

Cute little bipedal raptor-dragon → imposing four-legged celestial dragon.

Yorvale is intentionally wingless, emphasizing its identity as a powerful terrestrial dragon.

65–66 — Thessa → Thessane

Affinity: Mystic → Mystic/Frost Rarity: Common → Rare Concept: Curious mystical creature → elegant frost oracle

065 — Thessa

Average Height: .50 m Average Length: .40 m Size Class: Small

Thessa has a humanoid-inspired silhouette while remaining clearly non-human and creature-like.

  • Small bipedal creature with a delicate body.
  • Large head relative to its body.
  • Huge expressive eyes.
  • Long floppy ears or ribbon-like sensory appendages.
  • Short arms and legs.
  • Small three-fingered hands.
  • Soft, pale body with subtle markings.
  • A small crystal-like growth sits on its forehead.
  • It often holds its hands together while concentrating.
  • Tiny objects may occasionally float around it without it realizing.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet and observant.
  • Easily fascinated by books, symbols and unfamiliar objects.
  • Sometimes seems to stare into empty space as though seeing something others cannot.
  • Very empathetic and sensitive to its surroundings.

Mystic affinity:

  • Weak telekinesis.
  • Can sense unusual energy.
  • Produces tiny illusions.
  • Occasionally predicts immediate physical events through instinct rather than deliberate foresight.

066 — Thessane

Average Height: 1.20 m Average Length: .75 m Size Class: Large

  • Becomes taller and more refined.
  • Body remains humanoid but retains unmistakably non-human proportions.
  • Long flowing sensory appendages frame its head.
  • Forehead crystal becomes larger and more sophisticated.
  • Frost crystals develop along its arms, shoulders and lower body.
  • A delicate layer of frost constantly surrounds its feet.
  • Several small crystals float around it when using Mystic abilities.
  • Eyes become luminous and highly expressive.
  • Its movements become calm and deliberate.
  • Could have a subtle robe-like silhouette formed naturally from long crystalline/frost structures rather than actual clothing.

Mystic: telekinesis, barriers, illusions and energy manipulation. Frost: freezing energy, ice constructs and temperature manipulation. Overall progression:

Cute psychic little creature → elegant mystical frost oracle.

67–68 — Orli → Orlith

Wild → Wild/Metal Orli is an artifact-like creature, replacing the earlier arboreal concept.

067 — Orli

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .50 m Size Class: Small

Species concept: Tiny mysterious living artifact.

  • Small, floating artifact-like creature.
  • Roughly the size of a large book or small shield.
  • Body resembles an ancient polished stone or metal relic.
  • A central circular “eye” or gem gives it its expression.
  • Several small pieces orbit around the central body.
  • No conventional limbs.
  • The pieces subtly move in response to its emotions.
  • Small Wild-colored moss, vines or organic growths appear across portions of its surface, indicating that nature has grown around it.
  • Ancient-looking geometric markings cover its body.
  • The markings aren’t readable language—more like mysterious natural patterns.
  • Despite looking ancient, it has huge expressive eyes projected through its central gem.
  • It floats only a few centimeters above the ground.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet but inquisitive.
  • Fascinated by old ruins and artifacts.
  • Likes following explorers around.
  • Sometimes hides among ordinary ancient objects.
  • Its little floating fragments rearrange themselves when it gets excited.

Wild affinity: Rather than traditional plant powers, its Wild affinity is expressed through its symbiosis with nature.

  • Small plants grow around it.
  • It can encourage vines and moss to grow.
  • Can communicate with nearby plants in a rudimentary way.
  • Natural environments gradually “repair” its damaged exterior.

068 — Orlith

Average Height: .75 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

  • Larger, more elaborate artifact-like body.
  • Central core becomes substantially larger.
  • Floating fragments become numerous and more precisely controlled.
  • Wild growth becomes integrated into the structure rather than simply growing over it.
  • Vines can move independently around its body.
  • Metal affinity manifests as ancient metallic components emerging from within the artifact.
  • Some pieces form floating shields.
  • Others can form blades, chains or protective rings.
  • Its central eye/core becomes brighter.
  • Ancient markings illuminate when it uses its powers.
  • Despite becoming more powerful, it retains the appearance of a living relic rather than a robot.

Wild: control of organic growth and natural regeneration. Metal: manipulation of its metallic components and defensive/offensive constructs. Overall progression:

Tiny mysterious living relic → powerful ancient artifact surrounded by living nature and floating metal.

69–70 — Fenni → Fennovar

Affinity: Frost → Frost/Stone Rarity: Common → Rare

069 — Fenni

Average Height: .35 m Average Length: .55 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny frost mammal with an unusual body shape

  • Small, round-bodied creature with very short legs.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Small rounded ears.
  • A broad, fluffy tail that curls around its body when resting.
  • Thick pale fur with subtle crystalline structures embedded within it.
  • Small paws with slightly oversized pads, allowing it to walk across ice without slipping.
  • A little ridge of soft fur runs from its forehead down its back.
  • Mostly white and pale blue with darker markings around the ears and tail.
  • Its breath creates tiny snowflakes when it gets excited.

Behaviour:

  • Energetic and playful.
  • Loves rolling down snowy slopes.
  • Naturally seeks out cold environments.
  • Tends to curl itself into a little ball when sleeping.
  • Can instinctively sense approaching changes in temperature.

Frost affinity:

  • Produces freezing air.
  • Creates small patches of frost.
  • Can coat its paws in ice for short periods.

070 — Fennovar

Average Height: 1.10 m Average Length: 1.80 m Size Class: Large

  • Evolves into a much larger, sturdy creature.
  • Body becomes muscular and heavily built.
  • Fur becomes shorter across the torso while remaining thick around the neck and tail.
  • Natural Stone formations develop beneath and through its fur.
  • Forehead ridge becomes a substantial rocky crest.
  • Shoulders and legs develop stone-like armor.
  • Large paws become extremely powerful.
  • Tail becomes heavier and partially mineralized.
  • Frost gathers across its rocky armor, creating a combination of ice and stone.
  • Its movements remain surprisingly agile despite its increased size.

Frost: freezing attacks and ice creation. Stone: physical durability, heavy strikes and rock manipulation. Overall progression:

Cute snowy fluffball → heavily armored frost-and-stone beast.

71–72 — Caelo → Caelune

Gale → Gale/Tide Common → Uncommon

071 — Caelo

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .65 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny flying squirrel

  • Small quadrupedal flying-squirrel-inspired creature.
  • Large expressive eyes and rounded ears.
  • Soft, lightweight fur.
  • Gliding membrane between its limbs.
  • Long fluffy tail used for steering.
  • Small cheek and ear tufts.
  • Light gray, cream and pale blue coloration.
  • Extremely agile climber.
  • The Gale affinity naturally manifests through its gliding membrane and ability to catch tiny air currents.

Cute, energetic and very much a “playful and energetic” sort of creature.

072 — Caelune

Average Height: .70 m Average Length: 1.30 m Size Class: Medium

Concept: advanced flying squirrel with a tiny biological jetpack

  • Larger and more athletic than Caelo.
  • Much more developed gliding membranes.
  • Sleeker body and longer limbs.
  • Tail becomes broader and more aerodynamic.
  • Small jetpack-like structure develops naturally along its back.
  • Rather than technological machinery, the “jetpack” is a biological Gale/Tide organ: two compact vents that can blast compressed air and water vapor.
  • When activated, it produces a distinctive WHOOSH and launches Caelune forward.
  • Small fin-like structures around its limbs help stabilize it at high speed.
  • Can make surprisingly sharp aerial turns.
  • The membrane remains fully functional, meaning the jetpack supplements gliding rather than replacing it.
  • Its face stays cute and expressive, creating a funny contrast with its ridiculous aerial equipment.
  • The back-mounted structures could resemble two little aerodynamic pods.

Tide affinity could manifest through the moisture it draws from the air, producing condensed water vapor that gets expelled alongside the Gale energy.

73–74 — Rilsa → Rilsaryn

Verdant/Tide → Verdant/Tide Common → Rare The design emphasizes the flower association through its anatomy and coloration.

073 — Rilsa

Average Height: .40 m Average Length: .55 m Size Class: Small

Concept: cute flower creature

  • Small, delicate creature with a rounded body.
  • Four tiny legs hidden partially beneath layers of petals.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • A prominent flower bloom grows around its head, almost like a natural collar.
  • Soft green body with delicate stems and leaf-like appendages.
  • The flower has several overlapping petals with a gentle gradient of natural colors.
  • Small bud-like structures grow along its back.
  • Tiny droplets of water naturally collect on its petals.
  • Its petals subtly open and close depending on light and mood.
  • The creature should look like a living flower that happens to have a cute little animal underneath it, rather than an animal wearing a flower.

Behaviour:

  • Gentle and sociable.
  • Loves sunlight and shallow freshwater.
  • Often gathers in flower-filled meadows near ponds.
  • Its petals become more vibrant when it is happy.
  • Likes floating on water with its flower fully open.

Verdant: plant growth and manipulation. Tide: absorbs and stores water through its roots/stems.

074 — Rilsaryn

Average Height: 1.00 m Average Length: 1.40 m Size Class: Large

Now make the evolution beautiful rather than simply larger.

  • Becomes a graceful, elegant creature with a much more elaborate floral structure.
  • Body becomes slender and delicate.
  • The central flower expands dramatically into a layered bloom.
  • Multiple flowers develop along its back, shoulders and tail.
  • Long leafy structures flow from its body.
  • Its petals have a sophisticated, almost orchid/rose-like arrangement.
  • Tide energy manifests as tiny droplets and floating spheres of water around the flowers.
  • It can manipulate water to keep its flowers perfectly hydrated.
  • When using its strongest abilities, the flowers bloom simultaneously and release clouds of glowing pollen-like Verdant energy.
  • Its movements should resemble a gentle dance.
  • Could have a beautiful long tail ending in a large flower.

The important thing is that it shouldn’t become humanoid or overly fairy-like. It’s still a creature, just an exceptionally beautiful botanical one. Evolution progression:

Cute little flower creature → breathtaking living floral guardian.

75–76 — Uvera → Uveryn

Affinity: Mystic → Mystic/Dragon Rarity: Common → Extremely Rare

075 — Uvera

Average Height: .25 m Average Length: .75 m Size Class: Small

Concept: tiny mystical serpent

  • Small, floating serpentine creature.
  • No legs.
  • Slender body that coils naturally while hovering.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Small rounded horns or antenna-like protrusions.
  • Tiny fin-like structures along its head and back.
  • Smooth scales with a subtle pearlescent sheen.
  • A small gem-like marking sits on its forehead.
  • It floats rather than flies, with its body forming loose spirals in the air.
  • Pale lavender, silver, cream and muted blue tones.
  • Tiny motes of Mystic energy constantly drift around it.

Behaviour:

  • Curious and intelligent.
  • Fascinated by unusual objects.
  • Often follows people it finds interesting.
  • Seems capable of understanding considerably more than it can communicate.
  • Likes curling around warm objects while sleeping.

Mystic affinity:

  • Telekinetic manipulation of small objects.
  • Minor illusions.
  • Can sense unusual sources of energy.
  • Its floating movement is caused by Mystic energy rather than conventional flight.

076 — Uveryn

Average Height: .65 m Average Length: 3.00 m Size Class: Large

  • Becomes a long, elegant serpentine dragon rather than a conventional four-legged dragon.
  • Much larger, but still extremely graceful.
  • Body becomes covered in overlapping iridescent scales.
  • Head develops a distinctly draconic shape.
  • Long swept-back horns.
  • Several elegant whisker-like sensory tendrils.
  • No wings.
  • No conventional legs.
  • Its body remains naturally airborne, supported by Mystic energy.
  • Long mane-like structures flow from its head and neck despite there being no wind.
  • Dragon energy manifests as luminous patterns beneath its scales.
  • A brilliant central gem develops within its forehead.
  • When it becomes fully charged, enormous rings of mystical energy can form around its body.
  • Its movement should feel almost like swimming through the air.

Mystic: telekinesis, spatial distortion, energy manipulation and illusions. Dragon: powerful draconic energy and immense physical/spectral strength. Overall progression:

Tiny floating mystical serpent → ancient, wingless celestial serpentine dragon.

77–80 — Veyli and Branching Evolutions

Evolution: Three possible second-stage outcomes from a common base species.

077 — Veyli

Average Height: .50 m Average Length: .80 m Size Class: Small

Wild — Common Veyli is an adorable, adaptable puppy-like canine that serves as the common base for three divergent evolutionary outcomes. The important visual through-line should be:

  • Same basic facial structure.
  • Same expressive eyes.
  • Same distinctive ears and tail.
  • Compact puppy proportions.
  • Natural Wild coloration.
  • Nothing overtly elemental.

Then each evolution takes that foundation somewhere completely different.

078 — Veylith

Average Height: 1.00 m Average Length: 1.50 m Size Class: Large

Flame — Uncommon Hellhound-inspired canine Not literally demonic—more like a creature that has adapted to extreme heat.

  • Lean, athletic canine body.
  • Longer legs and powerful paws.
  • Dark charcoal/black coat.
  • Deep crimson markings beneath the fur.
  • Glowing orange-red eyes.
  • Ears become tall and pointed.
  • Fur around the neck, shoulders and tail becomes jagged and flame-like.
  • The tail ends in a constantly burning plume.
  • Small flames occasionally leak from its mouth when it exhales.
  • Heat visibly shimmers around its body.
  • Claws become dark, almost obsidian-like.
  • Flame energy pulses through markings along its legs and spine.

The silhouette should immediately give “hellhound”, but still clearly retain Veyli’s underlying canine anatomy. Personality: fierce, energetic and highly protective of its companions.

Veyli → Veylith: playful puppy → intimidating hellhound.

079 — Veyrune

Average Height: .65 m Average Length: 1.35 m Size Class: Medium

Tide — Uncommon

  • Compact, muscular semi-aquatic canine body.
  • Short, sleek, waterproof coat.
  • Rounded muzzle with a cute seal-like nose.
  • Small rounded ears rather than pointed canine ears.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Broad front paws with subtle webbing.
  • Hind legs become more flipper-like while retaining enough structure for land movement.
  • Thick, flexible tail adapted for swimming.
  • Smooth blue-gray, cream and pearl coloration.
  • Small fin-like structures along the sides of its neck.
  • Body shape becomes somewhat streamlined and chunky, like a seal crossed with a playful water dog.
  • When swimming, it becomes incredibly agile.

Personality: playful, confident and extremely social.

Veyli → Veyrune: playful puppy → powerful aquatic seal-dog.

080 — Veyvara

Average Height: 1.40 m Average Length: 1.60 m Size Class: Large

Mystic — Rare Veyvara is the Rare branch, drawing on an Anubis-inspired regal jackal canine silhouette. Its mythic identity comes from its anatomy, posture and ceremonial design rather than dark coloration.

  • Tall, slender regal canine.
  • Long elegant limbs and an upright, composed posture.
  • Narrow, refined muzzle.
  • Very large upright ears inspired by ancient jackal imagery.
  • Sleek short coat rather than fluffy fur.
  • Long, elegant tail.
  • Pale ivory and warm cream primary coloration, with subtle pale-gold accents.
  • Soft lavender, silver or faint blue Mystic markings can appear across the face, neck and limbs.
  • Eyes become luminous and slightly otherworldly.
  • A small crystalline marking sits between the eyes.
  • Subtle Mystic patterns run along the face, neck and legs.
  • Long ornamental fur structures around the neck create an almost ceremonial silhouette.
  • Several small crystalline or geometric objects float around it when its psychic abilities are active.
  • Its paws barely seem to touch the ground when it moves.
  • Mystic energy occasionally trails behind its body like translucent ribbons.
  • When it concentrates, its ears and tail rise unnaturally still while objects around it begin floating.
  • The overall impression should be ancient, ethereal and mythic, rather than dark, sinister or funerary.

Its expression should be calm, intelligent and almost eerily perceptive rather than aggressive. Mystic abilities:

  • Telekinesis.
  • Psychic barriers.
  • Illusions.
  • Levitation.
  • Limited manipulation of objects at range.

Veyli → Veyvara: adorable puppy → sleek, regal psychic jackal-like guardian.

081 — Mavren

Average Height: .45 m Average Length: .80 m Size Class: Small

Umbral/Wild — Uncommon

Species concept: nocturnal scavenger

  • Small quadrupedal creature.
  • Large rounded ears.
  • Short dark fur.
  • Long flexible tail with a fluffy end.
  • Large reflective eyes.
  • Small claws designed for climbing.
  • Distinctive mask-like markings around the eyes.
  • Thin patches of membrane between its forelegs and body allow it to make short gliding jumps.
  • Umbral energy naturally gathers around its silhouette in darkness.
  • Wild markings resemble vines or branches.

Behaviour:

  • Sneaky, mischievous and extremely curious.
  • Loves shiny objects.
  • Excellent at getting into places it shouldn’t.
  • Mostly active at dusk and nighttime.
  • Sometimes follows travellers purely because it wants to see what they’re carrying.

Umbral: concealment and shadow movement. Wild: climbing, agility and heightened senses.

082 — Ossari

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .70 m Size Class: Small

Stone — Common

Species concept: living fossil

  • Small, low-to-the-ground creature.
  • Body resembles a living fossilized shell, but it isn’t based directly on a turtle or snail.
  • Several overlapping stone plates form its back.
  • Tiny head protrudes from the front.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Six short little legs.
  • Its body is asymmetrical in places, like a naturally formed geological specimen.
  • Fossil-like patterns run through its stone plates.
  • Small crystals occasionally grow from cracks in its shell.
  • Slow-looking but surprisingly capable of sudden bursts of movement.

Behaviour:

  • Calm and stubborn.
  • Likes collecting minerals.
  • Can spend hours sitting completely motionless.
  • Its favourite resting spots are ancient rock formations.

Stone: hardening its body, manipulating small stones and detecting geological structures.

083 — Keln

Average Height: .18 m Average Length: .25 m Size Class: Tiny

Metal — Common

Species concept: tiny living piece of machinery

  • Small, compact creature with a rounded body.
  • Four tiny legs.
  • Body is made from naturally occurring metallic plates, almost like biological armor.
  • Large circular eyes.
  • Small antenna-like structures on its head.
  • A little rotating metallic ring sits around its torso.
  • Small vents on its sides.
  • Joints have flexible metallic material rather than conventional skin.
  • Body produces tiny clicks and whirring sounds as it moves.
  • Silver, bronze and dark metallic tones.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely industrious.
  • Loves dismantling small objects and putting them back together.
  • Attracted to machinery.
  • Often collects discarded metal pieces.
  • Can become fascinated with household appliances.

Metal affinity:

  • Magnetism.
  • Can attract and repel small metal objects.
  • Uses nearby metal to reinforce its body.

It should be the kind of creature where people go: …while it is already disassembling their toaster.

084 — Veyro

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .90 m Size Class: Small

Gale/Volt — Uncommon

Species concept: aerial electric manta/bat-like creature

  • Small airborne creature with a broad, angular body.
  • Four limb-like wing structures extending from its body.
  • No conventional legs while flying.
  • Shape loosely inspired by a manta ray crossed with a bat, but with its own anatomy.
  • Large expressive eyes on the front of its body.
  • Long trailing tail.
  • Thin membrane structures connect its limbs.
  • Small metallic-looking sensory whiskers extend from its head.
  • Gale energy keeps it airborne.
  • Volt energy periodically crackles across its body.
  • Blue-gray, silver and pale yellow coloration.

Behaviour:

  • Highly energetic.
  • Loves thunderstorms.
  • Often flies directly into strong winds just because it can.
  • Stores static electricity while airborne.
  • Travels in loose groups during storms.

Gale: flight, air currents and aerial agility. Volt: electricity storage and discharge. When it becomes fully charged, its silhouette is surrounded by tiny arcs of electricity while wind streams visibly around its body.

085 — Sindra

Average Height: 1.60 m Average Length: .90 m Size Class: Very Large

Flame/Umbral — Rare

  • Lean, athletic bipedal canine/feline-like creature.
  • Digitigrade legs and powerful reverse-jointed stance.
  • Long arms ending in three clawed fingers.
  • Sleek dark charcoal/black body.
  • Crimson markings along the chest, arms, legs and face.
  • Long pointed ears.
  • Narrow muzzle and sharp canine-like facial structure.
  • Glowing orange-red eyes.
  • A mane-like structure around the head and shoulders that resembles smoldering flame mixed with wispy shadow.
  • Long tail ending in a dark flame.
  • Small claws become black and almost obsidian-like.
  • Flame doesn’t simply cover her body—it occasionally appears to flicker in and out of existence, reflecting the Umbral affinity.
  • When standing still, shadows around her seem slightly deeper than they should be.

The design uses an athletic bipedal silhouette and combat-ready posture while maintaining an original species identity. Overall concept: mysterious martial-arts hellhound.

086 — Pavren

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .65 m Size Class: Small

Wild — Common

Species concept: tiny armadillo-like forager

Pavren fills the roster’s role as a small ground-dwelling species.

  • Small, low-bodied creature.
  • Six short, sturdy legs.
  • Rounded head with a tiny snout.
  • Large curious eyes.
  • Overlapping natural plates cover its back.
  • Plates are organic, not Stone or Metal.
  • Long flexible ears.
  • Small digging claws.
  • Tail curls slightly upward.
  • Warm sandy brown, cream and muted green coloration.
  • Can curl itself partially into a ball when frightened, though its head and legs don’t completely disappear.

Behaviour:

  • Curious and persistent.
  • Excellent digger.
  • Builds interconnected underground burrows.
  • Eats roots, berries, insects and small minerals.
  • Groups can completely rearrange the soil around their habitats.

The Wild affinity is about adaptability and natural survival, rather than plant powers.

087 — Ilyra

Average Height: .18 m Average Length: .22 m Size Class: Tiny

Radiant — Common

Species concept: little living sunbeam

  • Small floating creature.
  • Roughly spherical central body.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • No conventional limbs.
  • Several thin, ribbon-like appendages of light extend from its body.
  • The body itself resembles a tiny glowing pearl or miniature sun.
  • Small pointed “ears” made entirely from condensed light.
  • Its brightness changes with its mood.
  • During the day it becomes almost translucent.
  • At night it gives off a gentle warm glow.
  • Pale gold, cream and soft white coloration.

Behaviour:

  • Cheerful and affectionate.
  • Naturally attracted to people and other creatures.
  • Often follows travellers at night.
  • Sleeps by dimming itself almost completely.
  • Groups of Ilyra sometimes gather around flowers or reflective surfaces.

Radiant:

  • Produces light.
  • Can create brief flashes.
  • Can illuminate large areas.
  • Can generate a small protective field of light around itself.

Cute without needing to be another furry animal.

088 — Ruun

Average Height: 2.15 m Average Length: 1.70 m Size Class: Huge

Frost/Umbral — Rare Dark yeti is MUCH better.

  • Large, powerful bipedal yeti-like creature.
  • Extremely broad shoulders and long arms.
  • Thick shaggy fur.
  • Large hands and feet adapted for snow.
  • Small, deep-set glowing eyes.
  • Short, broad muzzle.
  • Small rounded ears partially hidden beneath its fur.
  • Dark charcoal, blue-gray and near-black fur rather than traditional white.
  • Frost accumulates naturally across its shoulders, arms and back.
  • Long claws with a dark icy sheen.
  • Thick mane around its neck and upper back.
  • Its breath produces dense freezing mist.
  • Umbral energy manifests as dark wisps around its body, particularly at night.
  • When standing in a snowstorm, its silhouette can almost disappear into the darkness.

Behaviour:

  • Solitary and territorial.
  • Surprisingly intelligent.
  • Usually avoids settlements.
  • Protects its territory rather than actively hunting travellers.
  • Has an almost folkloric reputation among people living near its habitat.

Frost gives it the ice and cold abilities; Umbral lets it disappear into snowstorms, darkness and shadow. So:

Ruun = dark, mysterious snow yeti.

089 — Talvi

Average Height: .25 m Average Length: .45 m Size Class: Tiny

Frost — Common

Species concept: tiny snow hare

Talvi provides a classic cute rabbit-like species within the roster.

  • Small, compact body.
  • Very large ears.
  • Large dark eyes.
  • Short fluffy tail.
  • Thick white fur.
  • Tiny paws.
  • Pale blue markings around its ears and cheeks.
  • Ears accumulate a thin layer of frost.
  • When it hops, tiny snowflakes scatter from its feet.
  • In particularly cold environments, its fur becomes exceptionally fluffy.

Behaviour:

  • Nervous but playful.
  • Extremely fast.
  • Lives in snowy grasslands and mountain valleys.
  • Often travels in large groups.
  • Loves hiding underneath snow.

Frost: creates small patches of ice and snow. Nothing too complicated. Sometimes a species can just be a ridiculously cute snow bunny.

090 — Mirel

Average Height: .30 m Average Length: .50 m Size Class: Small

Tide/Verdant — Common

Species concept: little swamp creature

  • Small, squat amphibious body.
  • Four short legs.
  • Large round eyes.
  • Wide little mouth.
  • Smooth, slightly damp skin.
  • Small leafy frills around the head.
  • A thin tail resembling a young plant stem.
  • Tiny flowers occasionally bloom along its back.
  • Green, turquoise and yellow coloration.
  • Webbed feet.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely relaxed.
  • Loves warm ponds and marshes.
  • Often sits partially submerged for hours.
  • Wherever groups of Mirel live, aquatic vegetation becomes unusually healthy.

Tide: water manipulation and aquatic mobility. Verdant: stimulates plant growth and maintains aquatic ecosystems.

091 — Korri

Average Height: .18 m Average Length: .30 m Size Class: Tiny

Metal/Wild — Uncommon

Species concept: living metallic beetle

Korri uses a distinctly different insect body plan from Braska’s line.

  • Small beetle-like creature.
  • Six legs.
  • Rounded body.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • Hard metallic-looking carapace.
  • Two small antennae.
  • The carapace has natural geometric patterns.
  • Short wings are tucked beneath its shell.
  • Body coloration resembles aged bronze, copper and dark green metal.
  • Small patches of moss-like organic growth develop around its joints.

Metal:

  • Extremely durable shell.
  • Can magnetically pull small metal objects.
  • Can reinforce its legs with metallic energy.

Wild:

  • Excellent climbing ability.
  • Can survive in almost any environment.
  • Feeds on organic material and minerals.

Behaviour:

  • Industrious.
  • Constantly collects small pieces of metal and natural debris.
  • Builds little nests from whatever it finds.

92 — Avenn

Average Height: 0.16 m Average Length: 0.55 m Size Class: Tiny

Gale — Common Avenn is a flying insect, providing a distinct aerial silhouette from the roster’s other flying species. Avenn is specifically a dragonfly-inspired aerial insect, distinguishing it from the roster’s other elegant flying species.

  • Small, slender insectoid creature.
  • Six delicate legs, though the rear four are mostly used for landing and gripping surfaces.
  • Large multifaceted eyes, but stylized enough to remain cute.
  • Four transparent wings.
  • Long segmented abdomen.
  • Small antennae.
  • Metallic-looking natural shell in pale blue, silver and soft green.
  • Wings constantly vibrate at extremely high frequency.
  • Can hover completely stationary.
  • Can change direction almost instantaneously.
  • Tiny Gale currents visibly swirl around its wings when it accelerates.

Behaviour:

  • Extremely energetic.
  • Loves flying over lakes, rivers and forests.
  • Frequently performs aerial loops as a display of natural aerial play.
  • Often hunts tiny insects while flying.
  • Groups can create miniature swirling patterns when several Avenn fly together.

Gale affinity:

  • Exceptional aerial maneuverability.
  • Can generate small bursts of wind with its wings.
  • Can hover against strong winds.
  • Its speed comes primarily from manipulating airflow rather than raw wing power.

Its visual identity is cute rather than creepy, with large eyes, elegant wings and an expressive face.

Avenn = tiny dragonfly-like wind sprite.

093 — Selka

Average Height: .22 m Average Length: .40 m Size Class: Tiny

Tide — Common

Species concept: tiny aquatic cephalopod

  • Small, rounded aquatic creature with a soft, almost jelly-like body.
  • Eight short, flexible tentacle-like appendages.
  • Large expressive eyes positioned toward the front.
  • Small fin-like frills around the sides of its head.
  • Smooth blue-green body with pale cream markings.
  • Tentacle tips are slightly darker.
  • A small translucent crest runs along its back.
  • Can change the intensity of its coloration depending on its mood.
  • When startled, it briefly becomes almost transparent.

Behaviour:

  • Curious and playful.
  • Excellent at squeezing into tiny spaces.
  • Likes collecting shells and brightly coloured stones.
  • Often hides inside coral formations.
  • Can propel itself through water by rapidly expelling water from beneath its body.

Tide affinity:

  • Water propulsion.
  • Small water jets.
  • Can temporarily alter its buoyancy.

Tiny aquatic mischief-maker.

094 — Norrik

Average Height: .60 m Average Length: 1.00 m Size Class: Medium

Stone/Metal — Uncommon

Species concept: living mining creature

Norrik is designed as a creature that naturally belongs underground, rather than as a conventional rocky beast.

  • Compact, heavily built mole-like creature.
  • Four powerful legs.
  • Large shovel-like front claws.
  • Small eyes adapted to darkness.
  • Short rounded ears.
  • Broad nose.
  • Back is covered in overlapping stone plates.
  • Thin metallic veins run through the plates.
  • Claws contain naturally formed metal deposits.
  • Dark gray, brown and muted copper coloration.
  • Small mineral crystals grow around its shoulders.

Behaviour:

  • Constantly digs.
  • Creates extensive underground tunnel systems.
  • Uses vibrations through its claws and body to detect underground structures.
  • Collects interesting minerals and metals.
  • Generally peaceful unless its territory is disturbed.

Stone: digging, geological sensing and rock manipulation. Metal: metallic claws and mineralized armor.

095 — Veysha

Average Height: 1.50 m Average Length: .85 m Size Class: Very Large

Mystic/Umbral — Rare

Species concept: masked spectral creature

  • Small-to-medium bipedal creature.
  • Slender body with long arms and digitigrade legs.
  • Head is almost entirely covered by a smooth, pale mask-like structure.
  • Two large luminous eyes visible through the mask.
  • No obvious mouth when viewed from the front.
  • Long dark tendrils of fur or energy extend from the back of its head.
  • Body is dark violet, charcoal and deep blue.
  • Pale markings form symmetrical patterns around the arms and legs.
  • Its shadow doesn’t always perfectly match its movements.
  • Several small pieces of its body can briefly become translucent.

Behaviour:

  • Quiet and highly observant.
  • Prefers abandoned structures and forests at night.
  • Doesn’t normally approach strangers.
  • Seems capable of sensing emotions.
  • Occasionally mimics sounds it has heard.

Mystic:

  • Telepathy at short range.
  • Illusions.
  • Mental perception.

Umbral:

  • Manipulation of shadows.
  • Concealment.
  • Can briefly separate from its own shadow, allowing it to scout nearby areas.

The mask gives Veysha a recognizable visual identity without making it another generic dark creature.

A mysterious masked psychic that doesn’t always obey the rules of shadows.

096 — Orven

Average Height: .35 m Average Length: .50 m Size Class: Small

Verdant — Common Orven explores fungal biology, providing a distinct Verdant concept from the roster’s flower and plant-based species.

Species concept: adorable mushroom creature

  • Small, round-bodied creature.
  • Four short legs.
  • Large expressive eyes.
  • A broad mushroom-cap structure grows from its back and head.
  • The cap isn’t simply sitting on top—it is biologically integrated into its body.
  • Soft cream-colored body.
  • Mushroom cap in muted green and brown.
  • Small spots across the cap.
  • Tiny root-like tendrils around its feet.
  • When it gets excited, tiny mushrooms briefly sprout around it.
  • Its cap changes shape slightly depending on its environment.

Behaviour:

  • Gentle and somewhat shy.
  • Lives in damp forests.
  • Often travels in small groups.
  • Helps spread spores and seeds through its habitat.
  • Other plants grow unusually well around groups of Orven.

Verdant affinity:

  • Accelerated plant/fungal growth.
  • Spore dispersal.
  • Can create small patches of vegetation.

And unlike Rilsa, which is our beautiful floral creature, Orven is the cute little woodland fungus guy.

097 — Auralith

Average Height: 2.80 m Average Length: 1.90 m Size Class: Huge

Radiant / Mystic — Legendary

  • Tall, imposing, semi-humanoid silhouette, but clearly a creature rather than a person.
  • Body composed of smooth, pale stone-like material with subtle crystalline qualities.
  • Large, faceted central “core” embedded in its chest.
  • The core emits a soft golden-white aura.
  • Head is relatively small compared with its body, with no conventional facial features beyond two luminous eyes.
  • Long, powerful arms ending in simplified, stone-like hands.
  • Legs resemble enormous carved pillars, giving it an incredibly stable silhouette.
  • Several floating monolithic fragments orbit its shoulders, back and head.
  • Ancient geometric markings run across its body and periodically illuminate.
  • Parts of its body appear almost translucent when its Radiant energy intensifies.
  • It doesn’t have conventional armor—the entire body looks like an ancient sacred monument that came alive.

The floating fragments could rearrange themselves during combat or when Auralith uses its Mystic abilities. Radiant: immense light generation, barriers, purification and concentrated beams. Mystic: telekinetic manipulation of its orbiting stones, psychic perception and manipulation of the luminous aura surrounding its body. Auralith moves with slow, graceful and imposing weight, reinforcing its ancient presence. The name reflects its luminous aura and stone-like body: Aura + Lith → Auralith.

098 — Vaelora

Average Height: 1.90 m Average Length: 2.80 m Size Class: Very Large

Radiant / Volt — Legendary

  • Large, majestic avian body.
  • Long aerodynamic wings with multiple layers of feathers.
  • Broad wingspan and powerful talons.
  • Long, elegant tail feathers.
  • Sleek head with a prominent swept-back crest.
  • Sharp, intelligent eyes.
  • Predominantly deep blue, white and metallic-gold plumage.
  • Individual feathers have subtle luminous patterns.
  • Lightning naturally arcs between the tips of its wings.
  • Some of its longest feathers become almost blade-like.
  • When it spreads its wings completely, the feathers form an almost sunburst-like silhouette.
  • During flight, radiant light and electrical arcs trail behind it.
  • In a storm, its coloration becomes dramatically more luminous.

Its signature visual

Vaelora’s signature visual is a huge silhouette surrounded by lightning and golden light when descending from the sky. Radiant gives it the celestial/sun aspect. Volt makes it the living embodiment of atmospheric electricity. Unlike Auralith’s ancient and contemplative presence, Vaelora is free, powerful and untamable.

099 — Nethryss

Average Height: 2.70 m Average Length: 5.30 m Size Class: Huge

Umbral / Dragon — Legendary Nethryss is intended to have an exceptionally intimidating presence.

Species concept: skeletal armored dragon

  • Massive quadrupedal dragon.
  • Extremely broad, muscular body.
  • Long, heavy tail.
  • Large clawed limbs.
  • Huge head with an elongated, predatory skull-like structure.
  • Prominent horns sweeping backward.
  • Dark scales are visible beneath its armor.
  • Large sections of its body are covered by natural skeletal armor.
  • Rib-like structures protect its chest and abdomen.
  • Vertebrae-like armor plates continue along its spine.
  • Jaw has exposed, fang-like bone structures.
  • Long black claws.
  • Deep crimson or violet eyes burning from beneath its armored skull-like face.
  • Dark mist constantly emanates from gaps between its armor.
  • Its wings, if present, are small or vestigial rather than conventional dragon wings.

Drakoryn establishes the roster’s imposing bipedal Dragon/Volt progression, while Vaelora provides the spectacular flying Legendary archetype. Nethryss should feel like a walking nightmare from beneath the earth.

Skeletal armor

The key distinction is that this isn’t literally undead. Its skeleton has evolved externally reinforced structures, almost like an extreme biological armor system. So the ribs, spine and skull-like plates are living parts of its anatomy. That lets us retain the dragon’s biological nature while giving it the horrifying skeletal aesthetic.

Umbral

Its presence suppresses surrounding light. Dark mist spills from its body. Shadows appear unnaturally elongated around it.

Dragon

Pure physical power and devastating Dragon energy. Its head is particularly intimidating: A huge horned skull-like face, glowing eyes recessed deep beneath the brow, and an enormous jaw filled with uneven dark fangs.

If someone sees Nethryss in the dark, they probably remember the experience for the rest of their life.

100 — Orrytheon

Average Height: 3.40 m Average Length: 5.80 m Size Class: Huge

Mystic / Metal — Legendary

Species concept: The Ancient Astral Construct

Not a robot. Not artificial. A living creature whose body naturally developed around metallic and crystalline structures.

  • Massive quadrupedal creature with an unusual, almost symmetrical silhouette.
  • Body resembles a combination of an ancient beast and a living astronomical mechanism.
  • Large central torso.
  • Four powerful legs.
  • Long neck.
  • Head resembles a smooth metallic mask.
  • No conventional mouth visible.
  • Several luminous eyes arranged symmetrically across its face.
  • Enormous metallic plates cover its body.
  • Plates are separated by glowing Mystic energy.
  • Large ring-like structures float around its torso and neck.
  • Several smaller metallic fragments orbit its body.
  • Its tail consists of multiple segmented metallic structures that float slightly apart rather than connecting normally.
  • Colors: dark silver, platinum, muted gold and deep violet.
  • When it moves, the metallic components subtly shift and realign.

The defining feature is that: The rings aren’t machinery. They’re part of its biology. They behave almost like planetary rings around a living world.

Mystic

Orrytheon can manipulate space, perception and psychic energy on a limited scale.

Metal

Its body is extraordinarily durable and it can manipulate metallic matter.
